===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.disables(DiagnosticGroup)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 115-133
Documentation:

Method code:
115 |   @Override
116 |   public boolean disables(DiagnosticGroup group) {
117 |     nextSingleton:
118 |     for (DiagnosticType type : group.getTypes()) {
119 |       DiagnosticGroup singleton = DiagnosticGroup.forType(type);
120 | 
121 |       for (WarningsGuard guard : guards) {
122 |         if (guard.disables(singleton)) {
123 |           continue nextSingleton;
124 |         } else if (guard.enables(singleton)) {
125 |           return false;
126 |         }
127 |       }
128 | 
129 |       return false;
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     return true;
133 |   }

===== M0012 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateMinimumChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 790-805
Documentation:

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

Method code:
551 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
552 |     if (child == first) {
553 |       return null;
554 |     }
555 |     Node n = first;
556 |     while (n.next != child) {
557 |       n = n.next;
558 |       if (n == null) {
559 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
560 |       }
561 |     }
562 |     return n;
563 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken,int,int,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1134-1159
Documentation:
/**
   * Looks for a parameter type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @param lineno The line of the type expression.
   * @param startCharno The starting character position of the type expression.
   * @param matchingLC Whether the type expression starts with a "{".
   * @param onlyParseSimpleNames If true, only simple type names are parsed
   *     (via a call to parseTypeNameAnnotation instead of
   *     parseTypeExpressionAnnotation).
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1134 |   private Node parseAndRecordTypeNode(JsDocToken token, int lineno,
1135 |                                       int startCharno,
1136 |                                       boolean matchingLC,
1137 |                                       boolean onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1138 |     Node typeNode = null;
1139 | 
1140 |     if (onlyParseSimpleNames) {
1141 |       typeNode = parseTypeNameAnnotation(token);
1142 |     } else {
1143 |       typeNode = parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1144 |     }
1145 | 
1146 |     if (typeNode != null && !matchingLC) {
1147 |       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
1148 |     }
1149 | 
1150 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1151 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1152 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1153 | 
1154 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(
1155 |           typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endLineno, endCharno, matchingLC);
1156 |     }
1157 | 
1158 |     return typeNode;
1159 |   }

350 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
351 |       Preconditions.checkState(
352 |           n.isName()
353 |           || n.isString()
354 |           || n.isGetterDef()
355 |           || n.isSetterDef());
356 | 
357 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
358 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
359 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
360 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
361 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
362 |           (parent.isGetProp() &&
363 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
364 |       if (n.isName() || isProperty) {
365 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
366 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
367 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
368 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
369 |           if (assertOnChange) {
370 |             String name = n.getString();
371 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
372 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
373 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
374 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
375 |           }
376 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
377 |         }
378 |       }
379 |     }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1161-1233
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Token.name(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }
1233 |   }

===== M0041 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1426-1445
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1426 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1427 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1428 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1429 |     }
1430 | 
1431 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1432 |     Node n, n2;
1433 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1434 |          res == null && n != null;
1435 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1436 |       if (node2 == null) {
1437 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1438 |       }
1439 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1440 |       if (res != null) {
1441 |         return res;
1442 |       }
1443 |     }
1444 |     return res;
1445 |   }

   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
440 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
441 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
442 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
443 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
444 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
445 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
446 |         String inputName = input.getName();
447 | 
448 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
449 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
450 |         // will get an error down the line.
451 |         inputs.add(input);
452 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
453 |       }
454 |     }
455 |     return inputs;
456 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.referenceAliases()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1272-1281
Documentation:
/**
   * The NameAnalyzer algorithm works best when all objects have a canonical
   * name in the global scope. When multiple names in the global scope
   * point to the same object, things start to break down.
   *
   * For example, if we have
   * <code>
   * var a = {};
   * var b = a;
   * a.foo = 3;
   * alert(b.foo);
   * </code>
   * then a.foo and b.foo are the same name, even though NameAnalyzer doesn't
   * represent them as such.
   *
   * To handle this case, we look at all the aliases in the program.
   * If descendant properties of that alias are assigned, then we create a
   * directional reference from the original name to the alias. For example,
   * in this case, the assign to {@code a.foo} triggers a reference from
   * {@code b} to {@code a}, but NOT from a to b.
   *
   * Similarly, "instanceof" checks do not prevent the removal
   * of a unaliased name but an instanceof check on an alias can only be removed
   * if the other aliases are also removed, so we add a connection here.
   */
Method code:
1272 |   private void referenceAliases() {
1273 |     for (Map.Entry<String, AliasSet> entry : aliases.entrySet()) {
1274 |       JsName name = getName(entry.getKey(), false);
1275 |       if (name.hasWrittenDescendants || name.hasInstanceOfReference) {
1276 |         for (String alias : entry.getValue().names) {
1277 |           recordReference(alias, entry.getKey(), RefType.REGULAR);
1278 |         }
1279 |       }
1280 |     }
1281 |   }

===== M0074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 70-87
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
70 |   @Override
71 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
72 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
73 |       return false;
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
77 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
78 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
79 |     if (pos >= 0) {
80 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
81 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
82 |         return false;
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 | 
86 |     return isConstantKey(name);
87 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 181-941
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
181 |   boolean parse() {
182 |     int lineno;
183 |     int charno;
184 | 
185 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
186 |     JSTypeExpression type;
187 | 
188 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
189 |     skipEOLs();
190 | 
191 |     JsDocToken token = next();
192 | 
193 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
194 | 
195 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
196 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
197 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
198 |       token = blockInfo.token;
199 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
200 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
201 |       }
202 |     } else {
203 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
204 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
205 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
206 |         // what it was.
207 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
208 |       }
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
212 |     retry: for (;;) {
213 |       switch (token) {
214 |         case ANNOTATION:
215 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
216 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
217 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
218 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
219 | 
220 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
221 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
222 |             if (annotation == null) {
223 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
224 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
225 |             } else {
226 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
227 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
228 | 
229 |               switch (annotation) {
230 |                 case AUTHOR:
231 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
232 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
233 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
234 | 
235 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
236 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
237 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
238 |                     } else {
239 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
240 |                     }
241 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
242 |                   } else {
243 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
244 |                   }
245 |                   continue retry;
246 | 
247 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
248 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
249 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
250 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
251 |                   }
252 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
253 |                   continue retry;
254 | 
255 |                 case CONSTANT:
256 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
257 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
258 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
259 |                   }
260 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
261 |                   continue retry;
262 | 
263 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
265 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
266 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
267 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                     } else {
269 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
270 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
271 |                     }
272 |                   }
273 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
274 |                   continue retry;
275 | 
276 |                 case DEPRECATED:
277 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
278 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
279 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
280 |                   }
281 | 
282 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
283 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
284 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
285 | 
286 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
287 | 
288 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
289 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
290 |                   }
291 | 
292 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
293 |                   continue retry;
294 | 
295 |                 case INTERFACE:
296 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
297 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
298 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
299 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
300 |                     } else {
301 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
302 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
303 |                     }
304 |                   }
305 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
306 |                   continue retry;
307 | 
308 |                 case DESC:
309 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
310 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
311 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
312 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
313 |                     continue retry;
314 |                   } else {
315 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
316 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
317 | 
318 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
319 | 
320 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
321 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
322 |                     continue retry;
323 |                   }
324 | 
325 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
326 |                   String fileOverview = "";
327 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
328 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
329 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
330 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
331 | 
332 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
333 | 
334 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
335 |                   } else {
336 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
337 |                   }
338 | 
339 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
340 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
341 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
342 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
343 |                   }
344 |                   continue retry;
345 | 
346 |                 case LICENSE:
347 |                 case PRESERVE:
348 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
349 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
350 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
351 | 
352 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
353 | 
354 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
355 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
356 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
357 |                     }
358 |                   }
359 | 
360 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
361 |                   continue retry;
362 | 
363 |                 case ENUM:
364 |                   token = next();
365 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
366 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
367 | 
368 |                   type = null;
369 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
370 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
371 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
372 |                   }
373 | 
374 |                   if (type == null) {
375 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
376 |                   }
377 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
378 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
379 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
380 |                   }
381 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
382 |                   continue retry;
383 | 
384 |                 case EXPORT:
385 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
386 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
387 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
388 |                   }
389 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
390 |                   continue retry;
391 | 
392 |                 case EXTERNS:
393 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
394 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
395 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
396 |                   }
397 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
398 |                   continue retry;
399 | 
400 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
401 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
402 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
403 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
404 |                   }
405 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
406 |                   continue retry;
407 | 
408 |                 case EXTENDS:
409 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
410 |                   skipEOLs();
411 |                   token = next();
412 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
413 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
414 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
415 | 
416 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
417 |                     token = next();
418 |                     matchingRc = true;
419 |                   }
420 | 
421 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
422 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
423 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
424 | 
425 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
426 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
427 | 
428 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
429 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
430 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
431 |                     }
432 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
433 | 
434 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
435 |                       // record the extended type, check later
436 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
437 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
438 |                     } else {
439 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
440 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
441 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
442 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
443 |                             lineno, charno);
444 |                       }
445 |                     }
446 |                     token = next();
447 |                     if (matchingRc) {
448 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
449 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
450 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
451 |                       }
452 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
453 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
454 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
455 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
456 |                     }
457 |                   } else {
458 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
459 |                   }
460 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
461 |                   continue retry;
462 | 
463 |                 case HIDDEN:
464 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
465 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
466 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
467 |                   }
468 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
469 |                   continue retry;
470 | 
471 |                 case LENDS:
472 |                   skipEOLs();
473 | 
474 |                   matchingRc = false;
475 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
476 |                     token = next();
477 |                     matchingRc = true;
478 |                   }
479 | 
480 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
481 |                     token = next();
482 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordLends(stream.getString())) {
483 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.incompatible",
484 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
485 |                     }
486 |                   } else {
487 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.lends.missing",
488 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
489 |                   }
490 | 
491 |                   if (matchingRc && !match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
492 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
493 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
494 |                   }
495 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
496 |                   continue retry;
497 | 
498 |                 case MEANING:
499 |                   ExtractionInfo meaningInfo =
500 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
501 |                   String meaning = meaningInfo.string;
502 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;
503 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordMeaning(meaning)) {
504 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.meaning.extra",
505 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
506 |                   }
507 |                   continue retry;
508 | 
509 |                 case NO_ALIAS:

601 |                       // recovering parsing

638 |                       } else if (type != null) {

640 |                         // already.

642 |                       }

646 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
647 |                     // for handling properties of params.

649 |                       name = null;

651 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
652 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,

656 |                             lineno, charno);
657 |                       }

663 |                     continue retry;
664 |                   }

667 | 
668 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
669 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
670 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
671 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);

673 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;

677 |                           paramDescription);
678 |                     }

681 |                   } else {

683 |                   }
684 |                   continue retry;
685 |

688 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
689 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
690 |                   }

692 |                   continue retry;

698 |                   }

886 |                         }

893 |                         }

907 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
908 |           }

914 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);

916 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

922 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
923 |           }

927 |         default:

929 |             token = next();
930 |             continue retry;
931 |           } else {
932 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
933 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
934 |             continue retry;
935 |           }
936 |       }
937 | 
938 |       // next token
939 |       token = next();

===== M0081 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-728
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
104 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
105 | 
106 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
107 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
108 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
109 | 
110 |       // Handle associativity.
111 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
112 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
113 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
114 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
119 |       } else {
120 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
121 |       }
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
126 | 
127 |     switch (type) {
128 |       case Token.TRY: {
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
130 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
132 | 
133 |         add("try");
134 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
135 | 
136 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
137 |         // isn't a catch block
138 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
139 |         if (catchblock != null) {
140 |           add(catchblock);
141 |         }
142 | 
143 |         if (childCount == 3) {
144 |           add("finally");
145 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         }
147 |         break;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       case Token.CATCH:
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
152 |         add("catch(");
153 |         add(first);
154 |         add(")");
155 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.THROW:
159 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |         add("throw");
161 |         add(first);
162 | 
163 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
164 |         // parse this.
165 |         cc.endStatement(true);
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.RETURN:
169 |         add("return");
170 |         if (childCount == 1) {
171 |           add(first);
172 |         } else {
173 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
174 |         }
175 |         cc.endStatement();
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.VAR:
179 |         if (first != null) {
180 |           add("var ");
181 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
182 |         }
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
186 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
187 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
188 |         break;
189 | 
190 |       case Token.NAME:
191 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |         } else {
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
195 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
196 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
197 |           if (first.isComma()) {
198 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
199 |           } else {
200 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
201 |             // precedence.
202 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
203 |           }
204 |         }
205 |         break;
206 | 
207 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
208 |         add("[");
209 |         addArrayList(first);
210 |         add("]");
211 |         break;
212 | 
213 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
214 |         add("(");
215 |         addList(first);
216 |         add(")");
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.COMMA:
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
221 |         addList(first, false, context);
222 |         break;
223 | 
224 |       case Token.NUMBER:
225 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (!first.isString() ||
270 |             !last.isString()) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
286 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
287 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
288 |         }
289 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
290 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
291 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
292 |           add("(");
293 |         }
294 | 
295 |         add("function");
296 |         add(first);
297 | 
298 |         add(first.getNext());
299 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
300 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
301 | 
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add(")");
304 |         }
305 |         break;
306 | 
307 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
308 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
309 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
310 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
312 | 
313 |         // Get methods are unnamed
314 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
315 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
316 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
317 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
318 |           add("get ");
319 |         } else {
320 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
321 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
322 |           add("set ");
323 |         }
324 | 
325 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
326 |         String name = n.getString();
327 |         Node fn = first;
328 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
329 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
330 | 
331 |         // Add the property name.
332 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
333 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
334 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
335 |             // unicode escaped.
336 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
337 |           add(name);
338 |         } else {
339 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
340 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
341 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
342 |             cc.addNumber(d);
343 |           } else {
344 |             addJsString(n);
345 |           }
346 |         }
347 | 
348 |         add(parameters);
349 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
353 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
354 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
355 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
356 |         }
357 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
358 |         if (preserveBlock) {
359 |           cc.beginBlock();
360 |         }
361 | 
362 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
363 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
364 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
365 |                 !preserveBlock &&
366 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
367 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
368 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
369 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
370 | 
371 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
372 |           if (c.isVar()) {
373 |             cc.endStatement();
374 |           }
375 | 
376 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
377 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
381 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
382 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
383 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
384 |           }
385 |         }
386 |         if (preserveBlock) {
387 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
388 |         }
389 |         break;
390 |       }
391 | 
392 |       case Token.FOR:
393 |         if (childCount == 4) {
394 |           add("for(");
395 |           if (first.isVar()) {
396 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
397 |           } else {
398 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           }
400 |           add(";");
401 |           add(first.getNext());
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
404 |           add(")");
405 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
406 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
407 |         } else {
408 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
409 |           add("for(");
410 |           add(first);
411 |           add("in");
412 |           add(first.getNext());
413 |           add(")");
414 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
415 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
416 |         }
417 |         break;
418 | 
419 |       case Token.DO:
420 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
421 |         add("do");
422 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
423 |         add("while(");
424 |         add(last);
425 |         add(")");
426 |         cc.endStatement();
427 |         break;
428 | 
429 |       case Token.WHILE:
430 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
431 |         add("while(");
432 |         add(first);
433 |         add(")");
434 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
435 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
436 |         break;
437 | 
438 |       case Token.EMPTY:
439 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(
444 |             childCount == 2,
445 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
446 |         Preconditions.checkState(
447 |             last.isString(),
448 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
449 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
450 |         if (needsParens) {
451 |           add("(");
452 |         }
453 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add(")");
456 |         }
457 |         add(".");
458 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
459 |         break;
460 |       }
461 | 
462 |       case Token.GETELEM:
463 |         Preconditions.checkState(
464 |             childCount == 2,
465 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
466 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
467 |         add("[");
468 |         add(first.getNext());
469 |         add("]");
470 |         break;
471 | 
472 |       case Token.WITH:
473 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
474 |         add("with(");
475 |         add(first);
476 |         add(")");
477 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
478 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
479 |         break;
480 | 
481 |       case Token.INC:
482 |       case Token.DEC: {
483 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
484 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
485 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
486 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
487 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
488 |         if (postProp != 0) {
489 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
490 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
491 |         } else {
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |           add(first);
494 |         }
495 |         break;
496 |       }
497 | 
498 |       case Token.CALL:
499 |         // We have two special cases here:
500 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
501 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
502 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
503 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
504 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
505 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
507 |         // that must be preserved.
508 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
509 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
510 |           add("(0,");
511 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
512 |           add(")");
513 |         } else {
514 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
515 |         }
516 |         add("(");
517 |         addList(first.getNext());
518 |         add(")");
519 |         break;
520 | 
521 |       case Token.IF:
522 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
523 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
524 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
525 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
526 |           cc.beginBlock();
527 |         }
528 | 
529 |         add("if(");
530 |         add(first);
531 |         add(")");
532 | 
533 |         if (hasElse) {
534 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
535 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
536 |           add("else");
537 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
538 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
539 |         } else {
540 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
541 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
542 |         }
543 | 
544 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
545 |           cc.endBlock();
546 |         }
547 |         break;
548 | 
549 |       case Token.NULL:
550 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
551 |         add("null");
552 |         break;
553 | 
554 |       case Token.THIS:
555 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
556 |         add("this");
557 |         break;
558 | 
559 |       case Token.FALSE:
560 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
561 |         add("false");
562 |         break;
563 | 
564 |       case Token.TRUE:
565 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
566 |         add("true");
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
570 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
571 |         add("continue");
572 |         if (childCount == 1) {
573 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
574 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
575 |           }
576 |           add(" ");
577 |           add(first);
578 |         }
579 |         cc.endStatement();
580 |         break;
581 | 
582 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
583 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
584 |         add("debugger");
585 |         cc.endStatement();
586 |         break;
587 | 
588 |       case Token.BREAK:
589 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
590 |         add("break");
591 |         if (childCount == 1) {
592 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
593 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
594 |           }
595 |           add(" ");
596 |           add(first);
597 |         }
598 |         cc.endStatement();
599 |         break;
600 | 
601 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
602 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
603 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
604 |         cc.endStatement();
605 |         break;
606 | 
607 |       case Token.NEW:
608 |         add("new ");
609 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
610 | 
611 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
612 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
613 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
614 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
615 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
616 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
617 |         }
618 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
619 | 
620 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
621 |         Node next = first.getNext();
622 |         if (next != null) {
623 |           add("(");
624 |           addList(next);
625 |           add(")");
626 |         }
627 |         break;
628 | 
629 |       case Token.STRING:
630 |         if (childCount !=
631 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
632 |               n.getParent().isObjectLit()) ? 1 : 0)) {
633 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
634 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
635 |         }
636 |         addJsString(n);
637 |         break;
638 | 
639 |       case Token.DELPROP:
640 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
641 |         add("delete ");
642 |         add(first);
643 |         break;
644 | 
645 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
646 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
647 |         if (needsParens) {
648 |           add("(");
649 |         }
650 |         add("{");
651 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
652 |           if (c != first) {
653 |             cc.listSeparator();
654 |           }
655 | 
656 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
657 |             add(c);
658 |           } else {
659 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isString());
660 |             String key = c.getString();
661 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
662 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
663 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
664 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
665 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
666 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
667 |                 // were unicode escaped.
668 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
669 |               add(key);
670 |             } else {
671 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
672 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
673 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
674 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
675 |               } else {
676 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
677 |               }
678 |             }
679 |             add(":");
680 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
681 |           }
682 |         }
683 |         add("}");
684 |         if (needsParens) {
685 |           add(")");
686 |         }
687 |         break;
688 |       }
689 | 
690 |       case Token.SWITCH:
691 |         add("switch(");
692 |         add(first);
693 |         add(")");
694 |         cc.beginBlock();
695 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
696 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
697 |         break;
698 | 
699 |       case Token.CASE:
700 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
701 |         add("case ");
702 |         add(first);
703 |         addCaseBody(last);
704 |         break;
705 | 
706 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
707 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
708 |         add("default");
709 |         addCaseBody(first);
710 |         break;
711 | 
712 |       case Token.LABEL:
713 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
714 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
715 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
716 |         }
717 |         add(first);
718 |         add(":");
719 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
720 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
721 |         break;
722 | 
723 |       default:
724 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
725 |     }
726 | 
727 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
728 |   }

===== M0100 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.addChildBefore(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 650-665
Documentation:
/**
   * Add 'child' before 'node'.
   */
Method code:
650 |   public void addChildBefore(Node newChild, Node node) {
651 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node != null,
652 |         "The existing child node of the parent should not be null.");
653 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
654 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
655 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
656 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
657 |     if (first == node) {
658 |       newChild.parent = this;
659 |       newChild.next = first;
660 |       first = newChild;
661 |       return;
662 |     }
663 |     Node prev = getChildBefore(node);

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1085-1105
Documentation:

Method code:
1085 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1086 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1087 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1088 |       return s;
1089 |     }
1090 | 
1091 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1092 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1093 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1094 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1095 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1096 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1097 |       // crazy.
1098 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1099 |         sb.append(c);
1100 |       } else {
1101 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1102 |       }
1103 |     }
1104 |     return sb.toString();
1105 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether this node is strictly on the left hand side of an assign
   * or var initialization. Notably, this does not include all L-values, only
   * statements where the node is used only as an L-value.

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PhaseOptimizer.consume(List<PassFactory>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PhaseOptimizer.java
Original range: 113-134
Documentation:
/**
   * Add the passes generated by the given factories to the compile sequence.
   *
   * Automatically pulls multi-run passes into fixed point loops. If there
   * are 2 or more multi-run passes in a row, they will run together in
   * the same fixed point loop. If A and B are in the same fixed point loop,
   * the loop will continue to run both A and B until both are finished
   * making changes.
   *
   * Other than that, the PhaseOptimizer is free to tweak the order and
   * frequency of multi-run passes in a fixed-point loop.
   */
Method code:
113 |   void consume(List<PassFactory> factories) {
114 |     Loop currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
115 |     boolean isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
116 |     for (PassFactory factory : factories) {
117 |       if (factory.isOneTimePass()) {
118 |         if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
119 |           passes.add(currentLoop);
120 | 
121 |           currentLoop = new LoopInternal();
122 |           isCurrentLoopPopulated = false;
123 |         }
124 |         addOneTimePass(factory);
125 |       } else {
126 |         currentLoop.addLoopedPass(factory);
127 |         isCurrentLoopPopulated = true;
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     if (isCurrentLoopPopulated) {
132 |       passes.add(currentLoop);
133 |     }
134 |   }

   * Extracts the line number and character number from a merged line char
   * number (see {@link #mergeLineCharNo(int, int)}).

Method code:
1191 |   protected static int extractLineno(int lineCharNo) {
1192 |     if (lineCharNo == -1) {
1193 |       return -1;
1194 |     } else {
1195 |       return lineCharNo >>> COLUMN_BITS;
1196 |     }
1197 |   }

===== M0118 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getMainOptimizationLoop()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 676-705
Documentation:
/** Creates the passes for the main optimization loop. */
Method code:
676 |   private List<PassFactory> getMainOptimizationLoop() {
677 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
678 |     if (options.inlineGetters) {
679 |       passes.add(inlineSimpleMethods);
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
683 | 
684 |     if (options.inlineFunctions || options.inlineLocalFunctions) {
685 |       passes.add(inlineFunctions);
686 |     }
687 | 
688 |     boolean runOptimizeCalls = options.optimizeCalls
689 |         || options.optimizeParameters
690 |         || options.optimizeReturns;
691 | 
692 |     if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
693 |       if (options.deadAssignmentElimination) {
694 |         passes.add(deadAssignmentsElimination);
695 |       }
696 |       if (!runOptimizeCalls) {
697 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
698 |       }
699 |     }
700 |     if (runOptimizeCalls) {
701 |       passes.add(optimizeCallsAndRemoveUnusedVars);
702 |     }
703 |     assertAllLoopablePasses(passes);
704 |     return passes;
705 |   }

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
190 |       case Token.COLON:
191 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
192 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
193 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
194 |       case Token.DO:
195 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
196 |       case Token.EMPTY:
197 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
198 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
199 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
200 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
201 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
202 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
203 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
204 |         } else {
205 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
206 |                                           node.getClass() +
207 |                                           " type: " +
208 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
209 |         }
210 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
211 |       case Token.FALSE:
212 |       case Token.NULL:
213 |       case Token.THIS:
214 |       case Token.TRUE:
215 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
216 |       case Token.FOR:
217 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
218 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
219 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
220 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
221 |         } else {
222 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
223 |                                           node.getClass() +
224 |                                           " type: " +
225 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
226 |         }
227 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
228 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
229 |       case Token.GETELEM:
230 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
231 |       case Token.GETPROP:
232 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
233 |       case Token.HOOK:
234 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
237 |       case Token.LABEL:
238 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
239 |       case Token.LP:
240 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
241 |       case Token.NAME:
242 |         return processName((Name) node);
243 |       case Token.NEW:
244 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
247 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
248 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.RETURN:
252 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
253 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
254 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
255 |       case Token.STRING:
256 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
257 |       case Token.SWITCH:
258 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
259 |       case Token.THROW:
260 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.TRY:
262 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.CONST:
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {

271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

   *
   * @param name A qualified name (e.g. "x" or "a.b.c")
   * @param scope The scope in which {@code name} occurs
   * @param rootNameNode The NAME node for the first token of {@code name}
   * @return The name information, or null if the name is irrelevant to this
   *     pass
   */
Method code:
1432 |   private NameInformation createNameInformation(
1433 |       String name, Scope scope, Node rootNameNode) {
1434 |     // Check the scope. Currently we're only looking at globally scoped vars.
1435 |     String rootName = rootNameNode.getString();
1436 |     Var v = scope.getVar(rootName);
1437 |     boolean isExtern = (v == null && externalNames.contains(rootName));
1438 |     boolean isGlobalRef = (v != null && v.isGlobal()) || isExtern ||
1439 |         rootName.equals(WINDOW);
1440 |     if (!isGlobalRef) {
1441 |       return null;
1442 |     }
1443 | 
1444 |     NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1445 | 
1446 |     // If a prototype property or method, fill in prototype information.
1447 |     int idx = name.indexOf(PROTOTYPE_SUBSTRING);
1448 |     if (idx != -1) {
1449 |       nameInfo.isPrototype = true;
1450 |       nameInfo.prototypeClass = name.substring(0, idx);
1451 |       nameInfo.prototypeProperty = name.substring(
1452 |           idx + PROTOTYPE_SUBSTRING_LEN);
1453 |     }
1454 | 
1455 |     nameInfo.name = name;
1456 |     nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable =
1457 |         isExtern || isExternallyReferenceable(scope, name);
1458 |     return nameInfo;
1459 |   }

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.valueConsumedByParent(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1716-1739
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if the parent reads the value of a child expression
   * directly.  This is true children used in predicates, RETURN
   * statements and, rhs of variable declarations and assignments.
   *
   * In the case of:
   * if (a) b else c
   *
   * This method returns true for "a", and false for "b" and "c": the
   * IF expression does something special based on "a"'s value.  "b"
   * and "c" are effectivelly outputs.  Same logic applies to FOR,
   * WHILE and DO loop predicates.  AND/OR/HOOK expressions are
   * syntactic sugar for IF statements; therefore this method returns
   * true for the predicate and false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
1716 |   private boolean valueConsumedByParent(Node n, Node parent) {
1717 |     if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent)) {
1718 |       return parent.getLastChild() == n;
1719 |     }
1720 | 
1721 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1722 |       case Token.NAME:
1723 |       case Token.RETURN:
1724 |         return true;
1725 |       case Token.AND:
1726 |       case Token.OR:
1727 |       case Token.HOOK:
1728 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
1729 |       case Token.FOR:
1730 |         return parent.getFirstChild().getNext() == n;
1731 |       case Token.IF:
1732 |       case Token.WHILE:
1733 |         return parent.getFirstChild() == n;
1734 |       case Token.DO:
1735 |         return parent.getLastChild() == n;
1736 |       default:
1737 |         return false;
1738 |     }
1739 |   }

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1072-1093
Documentation:

Method code:
1072 |   static boolean nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(Node n, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
1073 |     if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
1074 |       return true;
1075 |     }
1076 | 
1077 |     switch(n.getType()) {
1078 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1079 |       case Token.DEC:
1080 |       case Token.INC:
1081 |       case Token.THROW:
1082 |         return true;
1083 |       case Token.CALL:
1084 |         return NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1085 |       case Token.NEW:
1086 |         return NodeUtil.constructorCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler);
1087 |       case Token.NAME:
1088 |         // A variable definition.
1089 |         return n.hasChildren();
1090 |       default:
1091 |         return false;
1092 |     }
1093 |   }

Documentation:

Method code:
288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 398-411
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
398 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
399 |       Node ret;
400 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
401 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
402 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
403 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
404 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
405 |       } else {
406 |         ret = transform(n);
407 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
408 |       }
409 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
410 |       return ret;
411 |     }

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 459-474
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
459 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
460 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
461 |       Set<String> directives = null;
462 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
463 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
464 |         if (directives == null) {
465 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
466 |         } else {
467 |           directives.add(directive);
468 |         }
469 |       }
470 | 
471 |       if (directives != null) {
472 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
473 |       }
474 |     }

===== M0178 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 489-512
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
489 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
490 |     switch (n.getType()) {
491 |       case Token.STRING:
492 |       case Token.NUMBER:
493 |       case Token.NULL:
494 |       case Token.TRUE:
495 |       case Token.FALSE:
496 |         return true;
497 |       case Token.NOT:
498 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
499 |       case Token.VOID:
500 |       case Token.NEG:
501 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
502 |       case Token.NAME:
503 |         String name = n.getString();
504 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
505 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
506 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
507 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
508 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
509 |     }
510 | 
511 |     return false;
512 |   }

===== M0179 =====

172 |     @Override
173 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
174 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
175 |       if (n.isName()) {
176 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
177 |           return;
178 |         }
179 | 
180 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
181 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
182 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
183 |         if (var != null) {
184 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
185 |         }
186 | 
187 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
188 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
189 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
190 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
191 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
192 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
193 |           if (assertOnChange) {
194 |             String name = n.getString();
195 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
196 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
197 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
198 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
199 |           }
200 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
201 |         }
202 |       }
203 |     }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1704-1730
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1704 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1705 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1706 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1707 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1708 |       skipEOLs();
1709 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1710 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1711 |       skipEOLs();
1712 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1713 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1714 |       skipEOLs();
1715 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1716 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1717 |       String string = stream.getString();
1718 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1719 |         skipEOLs();
1720 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1721 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1722 |         return newStringNode(string);
1723 |       } else {
1724 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1725 |       }
1726 |     }
1727 | 
1728 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1729 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1730 |   }

     * Record that the properties of this name have been written to.

Method code:
684 |     private void recordWriteOnProperties(String parentName) {
685 |       do {
