===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 935-994
Documentation:

Method code:
935 |   private void toString(
936 |       StringBuilder sb,
937 |       boolean printSource,
938 |       boolean printAnnotations,
939 |       boolean printType) {
940 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
941 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
942 |       sb.append(' ');
943 |       sb.append(getString());
944 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
945 |       sb.append(' ');
946 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
947 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
948 |       // toStringTree.
949 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
950 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
951 |       } else {
952 |         sb.append(first.getString());
953 |       }
954 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
955 |       sb.append(' ');
956 |       sb.append(getDouble());
957 |     }
958 |     if (printSource) {
959 |       int lineno = getLineno();
960 |       if (lineno != -1) {
961 |         sb.append(' ');
962 |         sb.append(lineno);
963 |       }
964 |     }
965 | 
966 |     if (printAnnotations) {
967 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
968 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
969 |         int type = keys[i];
970 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
971 |         sb.append(" [");
972 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
973 |         sb.append(": ");
974 |         String value;
975 |         switch (type) {
976 |           default:
977 |             value = x.toString();
978 |             break;
979 |         }
980 |         sb.append(value);
981 |         sb.append(']');
982 |       }
983 |     }
984 | 
985 |     if (printType) {
986 |       if (jsType != null) {
987 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
988 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
989 |           sb.append(" : ");
990 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
991 |         }
992 |       }
993 |     }
994 |   }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 766-803
Documentation:

Method code:
766 |     @Override
767 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {
768 |       if (literalNode.isDestructuring()) {
769 |         reportDestructuringAssign(literalNode);
770 |       }
771 | 
772 |       Node node = newNode(Token.OBJECTLIT);
773 |       for (ObjectProperty el : literalNode.getElements()) {
774 |         if (config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
775 |           if (el.isGetter()) {
776 |             reportGetter(el);
777 |             continue;
778 |           } else if (el.isSetter()) {
779 |             reportSetter(el);
780 |             continue;
781 |           }
782 |         }
783 | 
784 |         Node key = transformAsString(el.getLeft());
785 |         Node value = transform(el.getRight());
786 |         if (el.isGetter()) {
787 |           key.setType(Token.GETTER_DEF);
788 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
789 |           if (getFnParamNode(value).hasChildren()) {
790 |             reportGetterParam(el.getLeft());
791 |           }
792 |         } else if (el.isSetter()) {
793 |           key.setType(Token.SETTER_DEF);
794 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.isFunction());
795 |           if (!getFnParamNode(value).hasOneChild()) {
796 |             reportSetterParam(el.getLeft());
797 |           }
798 |         }
799 |         key.addChildToFront(value);
800 |         node.addChildToBack(key);
801 |       }
802 |       return node;
803 |     }

   */

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.functionCallHasSideEffects(Node,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 987-1043
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if calls to this function have side effects.
   *
   * @param callNode The call node to inspected.
   * @param compiler A compiler object to provide program state changing
   *     context information. Can be null.
   */
Method code:
987 |   static boolean functionCallHasSideEffects(
988 |       Node callNode, @Nullable AbstractCompiler compiler) {
989 |     if (!callNode.isCall()) {
990 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
991 |           "Expected CALL node, got " + Token.name(callNode.getType()));
992 |     }
993 | 
994 |     if (callNode.isNoSideEffectsCall()) {
995 |       return false;
996 |     }
997 | 
998 |     Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
999 | 
1000 |     // Built-in functions with no side effects.
1001 |     if (nameNode.isName()) {
1002 |       String name = nameNode.getString();
1003 |       if (BUILTIN_FUNCTIONS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(name)) {
1004 |         return false;
1005 |       }
1006 |     } else if (nameNode.isGetProp()) {
1007 |       if (callNode.hasOneChild()
1008 |           && OBJECT_METHODS_WITHOUT_SIDEEFFECTS.contains(
1009 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1010 |         return false;
1011 |       }
1012 | 
1013 |       if (callNode.isOnlyModifiesThisCall()
1014 |           && evaluatesToLocalValue(nameNode.getFirstChild())) {
1015 |         return false;
1016 |       }
1017 | 
1018 |       // Math.floor has no sideeffects.
1019 |       // TODO(nicksantos): This is a terrible terrible hack, until
1020 |       // I create a definitionprovider that understands namespacing.
1021 |       if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isName()) {
1022 |         if ("Math.floor".equals(nameNode.getQualifiedName())) {
1023 |           return false;
1024 |         }
1025 |       }
1026 | 
1027 |       if (compiler != null && !compiler.hasRegExpGlobalReferences()) {
1028 |         if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isRegExp()
1029 |             && REGEXP_METHODS.contains(nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1030 |           return false;
1031 |         } else if (nameNode.getFirstChild().isString()
1032 |             && STRING_REGEXP_METHODS.contains(
1033 |                 nameNode.getLastChild().getString())) {
1034 |           Node param = nameNode.getNext();
1035 |           if (param != null &&
1036 |               (param.isString() || param.isRegExp()))
1037 |           return false;
1038 |         }
1039 |       }
1040 |     }
1041 | 
1042 |     return true;
1043 |   }

===== M0234 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0241 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 577-605
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
577 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
578 |       JSDocInfo info;
579 |       int valueNodeType;
580 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
581 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
582 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
583 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
584 |           break;
585 |         case Token.VAR:
586 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
587 |           if (info == null) {
588 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
589 |           }
590 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
591 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
592 |           break;
593 |         default:
594 |           if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) {
595 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
596 |             valueNodeType = Token.FUNCTION;
597 |             break;
598 |           }
599 |           return false;
600 |       }
601 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
602 |       return info != null &&
603 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
604 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
605 |     }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 510-545
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
510 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
511 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
513 | 
514 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
515 | 
516 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
517 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
518 | 
519 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
520 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
521 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     curNode = n;
525 |     pushScope(n);
526 | 
527 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
528 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
529 |       // scope.
530 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
531 |     }
532 | 
533 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
534 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
535 | 
536 |     // Args
537 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
538 | 
539 |     // Body
540 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
541 |             body.isBlock());
542 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
543 | 
544 |     popScope();
545 |   }

===== M0251 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.compileInternal()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 645-685
Documentation:

Method code:
645 |   private void compileInternal() {
646 |     parse();
647 |     if (hasErrors()) {
648 |       return;
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     if (!precheck()) {
652 |       return;
653 |     }
654 | 
655 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
656 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Move this into an instrument() phase maybe?
657 |       check();
658 |       return;
659 |     }
660 | 
661 |     if (!options.skipAllPasses) {
662 |       check();
663 |       if (hasErrors()) {
664 |         return;
665 |       }
666 | 
667 |       if (options.isExternExportsEnabled()
668 |           || options.externExportsPath != null) {
669 |         externExports();
670 |       }
671 | 
672 |       // IDE-mode is defined to stop here, before the heavy rewriting begins.
673 |       if (!options.ideMode) {
674 |         optimize();
675 |       }
676 |     }
677 | 
678 |     if (options.recordFunctionInformation) {
679 |       recordFunctionInformation();
680 |     }
681 | 
682 |     if (options.devMode == DevMode.START_AND_END) {
683 |       runSanityCheck();
684 |     }
685 |   }

   * Marks the end of a pass.

187 |   private void endPass(Node externs, Node root) {
188 |     Preconditions.checkState(currentTracer != null && currentPassName != null);
189 |

191 |     try {
192 |       stopTracer(currentTracer, currentPassName);

===== M0272 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.collectDefines(Node,GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 172-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds all defines, and creates a {@link DefineInfo} data structure for
   * each one.
   * @return A map of {@link DefineInfo} structures, keyed by name.
   */
Method code:
172 |   private Map<String, DefineInfo> collectDefines(Node root,
173 |       GlobalNamespace namespace) {
174 |     // Find all the global names with a @define annotation
175 |     List<Name> allDefines = Lists.newArrayList();
176 |     for (Name name : namespace.getNameIndex().values()) {
177 |       Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
178 |       if (name.docInfo != null && name.docInfo.isDefine()) {
179 |         // Process defines should not depend on check types being enabled,
180 |         // so we look for the JSDoc instead of the inferred type.
181 |         if (isValidDefineType(name.docInfo.getType())) {
182 |           allDefines.add(name);
183 |         } else {
184 |           JSError error = JSError.make(
185 |               decl.getSourceName(),
186 |               decl.node, INVALID_DEFINE_TYPE_ERROR);
187 |           compiler.report(error);
188 |         }
189 |       } else {
190 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
191 |           if (ref == decl) {
192 |             // Declarations were handled above.
193 |             continue;
194 |           }
195 | 
196 |           Node n = ref.node;
197 |           Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
198 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
199 |           if (info == null &&
200 |               parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
201 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
202 |           }
203 | 
204 |           if (info != null && info.isDefine()) {
205 |             allDefines.add(name);
206 |             break;
207 |           }
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     CollectDefines pass = new CollectDefines(compiler, allDefines);
213 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, pass);
214 |     return pass.getAllDefines();
215 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-156
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST: {
127 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
128 | 
129 |           // Add the function parameters
130 |           for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
131 |             String name = c.getString();
132 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
133 |           }
134 | 
135 |           // Add the function body declarations
136 |           Node functionBody = n.getNext();
137 |           findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
138 | 
139 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
140 |         }
141 |         break;
142 | 
143 |       case Token.CATCH:
144 |         {
145 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
146 | 
147 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
148 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
149 | 
150 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
151 |         }
152 |         break;
153 |     }
154 | 
155 |     return true;
156 |   }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.LinkedDirectedGraph.isConnectedInDirection(N,Predicate<E>,N)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/LinkedDirectedGraph.java
Original range: 203-215
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {
208 |       if (outEdge.getDestination() == dNode2 &&
209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {
210 |         return true;
211 |       }
212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

   * Initializes compiler options. All options are disabled by default.
   *
   * Command-line frontends to the compiler should set these properties
   * like a builder.
   */
Method code:
762 |   public CompilerOptions() {
763 |     // Accepted language

765 |

767 |     acceptConstKeyword = false;

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleGet(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 643-693
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     */
Method code:
643 |     void handleGet(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name) {
644 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
645 | 
646 |       Ref.Type type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
647 |       if (parent != null) {
648 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
649 |           case Token.IF:
650 |           case Token.TYPEOF:
651 |           case Token.VOID:
652 |           case Token.NOT:
653 |           case Token.BITNOT:
654 |           case Token.POS:
655 |           case Token.NEG:
656 |             break;
657 |           case Token.CALL:
658 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
659 |                    ? Ref.Type.CALL_GET
660 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
661 |             break;
662 |           case Token.NEW:
663 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
664 |                    ? Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET
665 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
666 |             break;
667 |           case Token.OR:
668 |           case Token.AND:
669 |             // This node is x or y in (x||y) or (x&&y). We only know that an
670 |             // alias is not getting created for this name if the result is used
671 |             // in a boolean context or assigned to the same name
672 |             // (e.g. var a = a || {}).
673 |             type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
674 |             break;
675 |           case Token.HOOK:
676 |             if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
677 |               // This node is y or z in (x?y:z). We only know that an alias is
678 |               // not getting created for this name if the result is assigned to
679 |               // the same name (e.g. var a = a ? a : {}).
680 |               type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
681 |             }
682 |             break;
683 |           case Token.DELPROP:
684 |             type = Ref.Type.DELETE_PROP;
685 |             break;
686 |           default:
687 |             type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
688 |             break;
689 |         }
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, name, type);
693 |     }

===== M0294 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 768-815
Documentation:

Method code:
768 |     @Override
769 |     public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
770 |       if (parent == null) {
771 |         return true;
772 |       }
773 | 
774 |       // Gather the list of nodes that either have side effects, are
775 |       // arguments to function calls with side effects or are used in
776 |       // control structure predicates.  These names are always
777 |       // referenced when the enclosing function is called.
778 |       if (n.isFor()) {
779 |         if (!NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
780 |           Node decl = n.getFirstChild();
781 |           Node pred = decl.getNext();
782 |           Node step = pred.getNext();
783 |           addSimplifiedExpression(decl, n);
784 |           addSimplifiedExpression(pred, n);
785 |           addSimplifiedExpression(step, n);
786 |         } else { // n.getChildCount() == 3
787 |           Node decl = n.getFirstChild();
788 |           Node iter = decl.getNext();
789 |           addAllChildren(decl);
790 |           addAllChildren(iter);
791 |         }
792 |       }
793 | 
794 |       if (parent.isVar() ||
795 |           parent.isExprResult() ||
796 |           parent.isReturn() ||
797 |           parent.isThrow()) {
798 |         addSimplifiedExpression(n, parent);
799 |       }
800 | 
801 |       if ((parent.isIf() ||
802 |            parent.isWhile() ||
803 |            parent.isWith() ||
804 |            parent.isSwitch() ||
805 |            parent.isCase()) &&
806 |           parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
807 |         addAllChildren(n);
808 |       }
809 | 
810 |       if (parent.isDo() && parent.getLastChild() == n) {
811 |         addAllChildren(n);
812 |       }
813 | 
814 |       return true;
815 |     }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 123-151
Documentation:

Method code:
123 |   private void overrideDefines(Map<String, DefineInfo> allDefines) {
124 |     boolean changed = false;
125 |     for (Map.Entry<String, DefineInfo> def : allDefines.entrySet()) {
126 |       String defineName = def.getKey();
127 |       DefineInfo info = def.getValue();
128 |       Node inputValue = dominantReplacements.get(defineName);
129 |       Node finalValue = inputValue != null ?
130 |           inputValue : info.getLastValue();
131 |       if (finalValue != info.initialValue) {
132 |         info.initialValueParent.replaceChild(
133 |             info.initialValue, finalValue.cloneTree());
134 |         compiler.addToDebugLog("Overriding @define variable " + defineName);
135 |         changed = changed ||
136 |             finalValue.getType() != info.initialValue.getType() ||
137 |             !finalValue.isEquivalentTo(info.initialValue);
138 |       }
139 |     }
140 | 
141 |     if (changed) {
142 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     Set<String> unusedReplacements = dominantReplacements.keySet();
146 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(allDefines.keySet());
147 |     unusedReplacements.removeAll(KNOWN_DEFINES);
148 |     for (String unknownDefine : unusedReplacements) {
149 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(UNKNOWN_DEFINE_WARNING, unknownDefine));
150 |     }
151 |   }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 |     compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(acceptEs5);
126 | 
127 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
128 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
129 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.replaceTopLevelExpressionWithRhs(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1652-1699
Documentation:
/**
   * Simplify a toplevel expression, while preserving program
   * behavior.
   */
Method code:
1652 |   private void replaceTopLevelExpressionWithRhs(Node parent, Node n) {
1653 |     // validate inputs
1654 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1655 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1656 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1657 |       case Token.FOR:
1658 |       case Token.LABEL:
1659 |         break;
1660 |       default:
1661 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1662 |             "Unsupported parent node type in replaceWithRhs " +
1663 |             Token.name(parent.getType()));
1664 |     }
1665 | 
1666 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1667 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
1668 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1669 |       case Token.VAR:
1670 |         break;
1671 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1672 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(parent.isFor(),
1673 |             "Unsupported assignment in replaceWithRhs. parent: %s", Token.name(parent.getType()));
1674 |         break;
1675 |       default:
1676 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
1677 |             "Unsupported node type in replaceWithRhs " +
1678 |             Token.name(n.getType()));
1679 |     }
1680 | 
1681 |     // gather replacements
1682 |     List<Node> replacements = Lists.newArrayList();
1683 |     for (Node rhs : getRhsSubexpressions(n)) {
1684 |       replacements.addAll(getSideEffectNodes(rhs));
1685 |     }
1686 | 
1687 |     if (parent.isFor()) {
1688 |       // tweak replacements array s.t. it is a single expression node.
1689 |       if (replacements.isEmpty()) {
1690 |         replacements.add(IR.empty());
1691 |       } else {
1692 |         Node expr = collapseReplacements(replacements);
1693 |         replacements.clear();
1694 |         replacements.add(expr);
1695 |       }
1696 |     }
1697 | 
1698 |     changeProxy.replaceWith(parent, n, replacements);
1699 |   }

===== M0346 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 74-129
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
74 |   @Override
75 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
76 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
77 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
78 |     if (type != null) {
79 |       Node subclass = null;
80 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
81 | 
82 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
83 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
84 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
85 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
86 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
87 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
88 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
89 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
90 |           callName.isGetProp();
91 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
92 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
93 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
94 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
95 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
96 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
97 |       } else {
98 |         return null;
99 |       }
100 | 
101 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
102 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
103 |         // inheritance.
104 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
105 |           return null;
106 |         }
107 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
108 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
109 |             return null;
110 |           }
111 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
112 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
113 |         }
114 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
115 |       }
116 | 
117 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
118 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
119 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
120 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
121 |       if (subclass != null &&
122 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
123 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
124 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()

Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1547 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1548 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1549 |       return getString();
1550 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1551 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1552 |       if (left == null) {
1553 |         return null;
1554 |       }
1555 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1556 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1557 |       return "this";
1558 |     } else {
1559 |       return null;
1560 |     }
1561 |   }

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.VarCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/VarCheck.java
Original range: 120-203
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   @Override
121 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
122 |     if (!n.isName()) {
123 |       return;
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     String varName = n.getString();
127 | 
128 |     // Only a function can have an empty name.
129 |     if (varName.isEmpty()) {
130 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
131 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent));
132 |       return;
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     // Check if this is a declaration for a var that has been declared
136 |     // elsewhere. If so, mark it as a duplicate.
137 |     if ((parent.isVar() ||
138 |          NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent)) &&
139 |         varsToDeclareInExterns.contains(varName)) {
140 |       createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
141 | 
142 |       n.addSuppression("duplicate");
143 |     }
144 | 
145 |     // Check that the var has been declared.
146 |     Scope scope = t.getScope();
147 |     Scope.Var var = scope.getVar(varName);
148 |     if (var == null) {
149 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
150 |         // e.g. [ function foo() {} ], it's okay if "foo" isn't defined in the
151 |         // current scope.
152 |       } else {
153 |         // The extern checks are stricter, don't report a second error.
154 |         if (!strictExternCheck || !t.getInput().isExtern()) {
155 |           t.report(n, UNDEFINED_VAR_ERROR, varName);
156 |         }
157 | 
158 |         if (sanityCheck) {
159 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected variable " + varName);
160 |         } else {
161 |           createSynthesizedExternVar(varName);
162 |           scope.getGlobalScope().declare(varName, n,
163 |               null, getSynthesizedExternsInput());
164 |         }
165 |       }
166 |       return;
167 |     }
168 | 
169 |     CompilerInput currInput = t.getInput();
170 |     CompilerInput varInput = var.input;
171 |     if (currInput == varInput || currInput == null || varInput == null) {
172 |       // The variable was defined in the same file. This is fine.
173 |       return;
174 |     }
175 | 
176 |     // Check module dependencies.
177 |     JSModule currModule = currInput.getModule();
178 |     JSModule varModule = varInput.getModule();
179 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
180 |     if (!sanityCheck &&
181 |         varModule != currModule && varModule != null && currModule != null) {
182 |       if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(currModule, varModule)) {
183 |         // The module dependency was properly declared.
184 |       } else {
185 |         if (scope.isGlobal()) {
186 |           if (moduleGraph.dependsOn(varModule, currModule)) {
187 |             // The variable reference violates a declared module dependency.
188 |             t.report(n, VIOLATED_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
189 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
190 |           } else {
191 |             // The variable reference is between two modules that have no
192 |             // dependency relationship. This should probably be considered an
193 |             // error, but just issue a warning for now.
194 |             t.report(n, MISSING_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
195 |                      currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
196 |           }
197 |         } else {
198 |           t.report(n, STRICT_MODULE_DEP_ERROR,
199 |                    currModule.getName(), varModule.getName(), varName);
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     }
203 |   }

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 119-150
Documentation:

Method code:
119 |   @Override
120 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
121 |     new NodeTraversal(
122 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
123 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
124 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
125 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
126 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
127 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
128 |     }
129 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
130 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
131 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
132 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
133 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
134 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
135 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
136 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
137 |     //   function f() {
138 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
139 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
140 |     //   }
141 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
142 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
143 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
144 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
145 |         .process(externs, root);
146 | 
147 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
148 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
149 |     }
150 |   }

     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *

841 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {

     * Rename vars for the current scope, and merge any referenced
     * names into the parent scope reference set.
     */

313 |     @Override
314 |     public void exitScope(NodeTraversal t) {
315 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {

246 |       allRefInfo = Maps.newHashMap();
247 |       for (Name name : listOfDefines) {
248 |         Ref decl = name.getDeclaration();
249 |         if (decl != null) {
250 |           allRefInfo.put(decl.node,
251 |                          new RefInfo(decl, name));
252 |         }
253 |         for (Ref ref : name.getRefs()) {
254 |           if (ref == decl) {
255 |             // Declarations were handled above.
256 |             continue;
257 |           }
258 | 
259 |           // If there's a TWIN def, only put one of the twins in.
260 |           if (ref.getTwin() == null || !ref.getTwin().isSet()) {
261 |             allRefInfo.put(ref.node, new RefInfo(ref, name));
262 |           }
263 |         }
264 |       }
265 |     }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.createNameInformation(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1320-1420
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates name information for the current node during a traversal.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal
   * @param n The current node
   * @param parent The parent of n
   * @return The name information, or null if the name is irrelevant to this
   *     pass
   */
Method code:
1320 |   private NameInformation createNameInformation(NodeTraversal t, Node n,
1321 |       Node parent) {
1322 |     // Build the full name and find its root node by iterating down through all
1323 |     // GETPROP/GETELEM nodes.
1324 |     String name = "";
1325 |     Node rootNameNode = n;
1326 |     boolean bNameWasShortened = false;
1327 |     while (true) {
1328 |       if (NodeUtil.isGet(rootNameNode)) {
1329 |         Node prop = rootNameNode.getLastChild();
1330 |         if (rootNameNode.isGetProp()) {
1331 |           name = "." + prop.getString() + name;
1332 |         } else {
1333 |           // We consider the name to be "a.b" in a.b['c'] or a.b[x].d.
1334 |           bNameWasShortened = true;
1335 |           name = "";
1336 |         }
1337 |         rootNameNode = rootNameNode.getFirstChild();
1338 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(
1339 |           rootNameNode, rootNameNode.getParent())) {
1340 |         name = "." + rootNameNode.getString() + name;
1341 | 
1342 |         // Check if this is an object literal assigned to something.
1343 |         Node objLit = rootNameNode.getParent();
1344 |         Node objLitParent = objLit.getParent();
1345 |         if (objLitParent.isAssign()) {
1346 |           // This must be the right side of the assign.
1347 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent.getFirstChild();
1348 |         } else if (objLitParent.isName()) {
1349 |           // This must be a VAR initialization.
1350 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent;
1351 |         } else if (objLitParent.isString()) {
1352 |           // This must be a object literal key initialization.
1353 |           rootNameNode = objLitParent;
1354 |         } else {
1355 |           return null;
1356 |         }
1357 |       } else {
1358 |         break;
1359 |       }
1360 |     }
1361 | 
1362 |     // Check whether this is a class-defining call. Classes may only be defined
1363 |     // in the global scope.
1364 |     if (parent.isCall() && t.inGlobalScope()) {
1365 |       CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
1366 |       SubclassRelationship classes = convention.getClassesDefinedByCall(parent);
1367 |       if (classes != null) {
1368 |         NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1369 |         nameInfo.name = classes.subclassName;
1370 |         nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef = true;
1371 |         nameInfo.superclass = classes.superclassName;
1372 |         return nameInfo;
1373 |       }
1374 | 
1375 |       String singletonGetterClass =
1376 |           convention.getSingletonGetterClassName(parent);
1377 |       if (singletonGetterClass != null) {
1378 |         NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1379 |         nameInfo.name = singletonGetterClass;
1380 |         nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef = true;
1381 |         return nameInfo;
1382 |       }
1383 |     }
1384 | 
1385 |     switch (rootNameNode.getType()) {
1386 |       case Token.NAME:
1387 |         // Check whether this is an assignment to a prototype property
1388 |         // of an object defined in the global scope.
1389 |         if (!bNameWasShortened &&
1390 |             n.isGetProp() &&
1391 |             parent.isAssign() &&
1392 |             "prototype".equals(n.getLastChild().getString())) {
1393 |           if (createNameInformation(t, n.getFirstChild(), n) != null) {
1394 |             name = rootNameNode.getString() + name;
1395 |             name = name.substring(0, name.length() - PROTOTYPE_SUFFIX_LEN);
1396 |             NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1397 |             nameInfo.name = name;
1398 |             return nameInfo;
1399 |           } else {
1400 |             return null;
1401 |           }
1402 |         }
1403 |         return createNameInformation(
1404 |             rootNameNode.getString() + name, t.getScope(), rootNameNode);
1405 |       case Token.THIS:
1406 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
1407 |           NameInformation nameInfo = new NameInformation();
1408 |           if (name.indexOf('.') == 0) {
1409 |             nameInfo.name = name.substring(1);  // strip leading "."
1410 |           } else {
1411 |             nameInfo.name = name;
1412 |           }
1413 |           nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable = true;
1414 |           return nameInfo;
1415 |         }
1416 |         return null;
1417 |       default:
1418 |         return null;
1419 |     }
1420 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
158 |   @Override
159 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
160 |     switch (n.getType()) {
161 |       case Token.NAME:
162 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
163 |         if (newName != null) {
164 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
165 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
166 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
167 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);

170 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
171 |         }
172 |         break;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
175 |         // Remove the function body scope
176 |         nameStack.pop();
177 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
178 |         nameStack.pop();
179 |         break;

182 |         // Note: The parameters and function body variables live in the
183 |         // same scope, we introduce the scope when in the "shouldTraverse"
184 |         // visit of LP, but remove it when when we exit the function above.
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.CATCH:
188 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
189 |         nameStack.pop();
190 |         break;
191 |     }
192 |   }

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.getRhsSubexpressions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1766-1803
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a list of subexpressions that act as right hand sides.
   */
Method code:
1766 |   private List<Node> getRhsSubexpressions(Node n) {
1767 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1768 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
1769 |         // process body
1770 |         return getRhsSubexpressions(n.getFirstChild());
1771 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1772 |         // function nodes have no rhs
1773 |         return Collections.emptyList();
1774 |       case Token.NAME:
1775 |         {
1776 |           // parent is a var node.  rhs is first child
1777 |           Node rhs = n.getFirstChild();
1778 |           if (rhs != null) {
1779 |             return Lists.newArrayList(rhs);
1780 |           } else {
1781 |             return Collections.emptyList();
1782 |           }
1783 |         }
1784 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1785 |         {
1786 |           // add lhs and rhs expressions - lhs may be a complex expression
1787 |           Node lhs = n.getFirstChild();
1788 |           Node rhs = lhs.getNext();
1789 |           return Lists.newArrayList(lhs, rhs);
1790 |         }
1791 |       case Token.VAR:
1792 |         {
1793 |           // recurse on all children
1794 |           List<Node> nodes = Lists.newArrayList();
1795 |           for (Node child : n.children()) {
1796 |             nodes.addAll(getRhsSubexpressions(child));
1797 |           }
1798 |           return nodes;
1799 |         }
1800 |       default:
1801 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException("AstChangeProxy::getRhs " + n);
1802 |     }
1803 |   }

/**
   * Parse a {@code @modifies} tag of the form
   * {@code @modifies&#123;this|arguments|param&#125;}.
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   */
Method code:
1012 |   private JsDocToken parseModifiesTag(JsDocToken token) {
1013 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1014 |       Set<String> modifies = new HashSet<String>();
1015 |       while (true) {
1016 |         if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1017 |           String name = stream.getString();
1018 |           if (!modifiesAnnotationKeywords.contains(name)
1019 |               && !jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
1020 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.unknown", name,
1021 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1022 |           }
1023 | 
1024 |           modifies.add(stream.getString());
1025 |           token = next();
1026 |         } else {
1027 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1028 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1029 |           return token;
1030 |         }
1031 | 
1032 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1033 |           token = next();
1034 |         } else {
1035 |           break;
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 | 
1039 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1040 |         parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies",
1041 |             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1042 |       } else {
1043 |         token = next();
1044 |         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordModifies(modifies)) {
1045 |           parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.modifies.duplicate",
1046 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
1047 |         }
1048 |       }
1049 |     }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 508-530
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
508 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
509 |       switch (n.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
511 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
512 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
513 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
514 |         case Token.OR:
515 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
516 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
517 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
518 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
519 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
520 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
521 |         case Token.HOOK:
522 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
523 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
524 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);

Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
552 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
553 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
554 |         next = c.getNext();
555 |         if (c.isVar()) {
556 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
557 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
558 |           }
559 | 
560 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
561 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
562 |             c.removeChild(name);
563 |             Node newVar = IR.var(name).srcref(n);
564 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
565 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
566 |           }
567 |         }
568 |       }
569 |     }

===== M0442 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 58-194
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
58 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
59 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
60 |     int c;
61 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
62 |     for (;;) {
63 |       // eat white spaces
64 |       for (;;) {
65 |         charno = -1;
66 |         c = getChar();
67 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
68 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
69 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
71 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
72 |           break;
73 |         }
74 |       }
75 | 
76 |       switch (c) {
77 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
78 |         case '@':
79 |           do {
80 |             c = getChar();
81 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
82 |               addToString(c);
83 |             } else {
84 |               ungetChar(c);
85 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
86 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
87 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
88 |             }
89 |           } while (true);
90 | 
91 |         case '*':
92 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
93 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
94 |           } else {
95 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
96 |           }
97 | 
98 |         case ',':
99 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
100 | 
101 |         case '>':
102 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
103 | 
104 |         case '(':
105 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
106 | 
107 |         case ')':
108 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
109 | 
110 |         case '{':
111 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
112 | 
113 |         case '}':
114 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
115 | 
116 |         case '[':
117 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
118 | 
119 |         case ']':
120 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
121 | 
122 |         case '?':
123 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
124 | 
125 |         case '!':
126 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
127 | 
128 |         case ':':
129 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
130 | 
131 |         case '=':
132 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
133 | 
134 |         case '|':
135 |           matchChar('|');
136 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
137 | 
138 |         case '.':
139 |           c = getChar();
140 |           if (c == '<') {
141 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
142 |           } else {
143 |             if (c == '.') {
144 |               c = getChar();
145 |               if (c == '.') {
146 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
147 |               } else {
148 |                 addToString('.');
149 |               }
150 |             }
151 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
152 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
153 |             c = '.';
154 |           }
155 |           // fall through
156 | 
157 |         default: {
158 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
159 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
160 |           int c1 = c;
161 |           addToString(c);
162 |           int c2 = getChar();
163 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
164 |             ungetChar(c2);
165 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
166 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
167 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
168 |           } else {
169 |             do {
170 |               c1 = c2;
171 |               c2 = getChar();
172 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
173 |                 ungetChar(c2);
174 |                 ungetChar(c1);
175 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
176 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
177 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
178 |               } else {
179 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
180 |                   addToString(c1);
181 |                 } else {
182 |                   ungetChar(c2);
183 |                   addToString(c1);
184 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
185 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
186 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
187 |                 }
188 |               }
189 |             } while (true);
190 |           }
191 |         }
192 |       }
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0447 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1659-1698
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1659 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1660 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1661 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1662 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1663 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1664 |       // {?} - right curly
1665 |       // {?=} - equals
1666 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1667 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1668 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1669 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1670 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1671 |       token = next();
1672 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1673 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1674 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1675 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1676 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1677 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1678 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1679 |       }
1680 | 
1681 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1682 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1683 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1684 |     } else {
1685 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1686 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1687 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1688 |           next();
1689 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1690 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1691 |           next();
1692 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1693 |         }
1694 |       }
1695 | 
1696 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1697 |     }
1698 |   }

434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 |

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 784-927
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
784 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
785 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
786 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
787 |     // that we know to be safe
788 |     switch (n.getType()) {
789 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
790 |       case Token.AND:
791 |       case Token.BLOCK:
792 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
793 |       case Token.HOOK:
794 |       case Token.IF:
795 |       case Token.IN:
796 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
797 |       case Token.NUMBER:
798 |       case Token.OR:
799 |       case Token.THIS:
800 |       case Token.TRUE:
801 |       case Token.FALSE:
802 |       case Token.NULL:
803 |       case Token.STRING:
804 |       case Token.SWITCH:
805 |       case Token.TRY:
806 |       case Token.EMPTY:
807 |         break;
808 | 
809 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
810 |       case Token.THROW:
811 |         return true;
812 | 
813 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
814 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
815 |           return true;
816 |         }
817 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
818 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
819 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
820 |             return true;
821 |           }
822 |         }
823 |         return false;
824 | 
825 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
826 |       case Token.REGEXP:
827 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
828 |           return true;
829 |         }
830 |         break;
831 | 
832 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
833 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
834 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
835 |           return true;
836 |         }
837 |         break;
838 | 
839 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
840 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
841 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
842 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
843 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
844 | 
845 |       case Token.NEW:
846 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
847 |           return true;
848 |         }
849 | 
850 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
851 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
852 |           // side-effects
853 |           break;
854 |         }
855 |         return true;
856 | 
857 |       case Token.CALL:
858 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
859 |         // side effect property set.
860 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
861 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
862 |           // side-effects
863 |           break;
864 |         }
865 |         return true;
866 | 
867 |       default:
868 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
869 |           break;
870 |         }
871 | 
872 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
873 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
874 |           if (assignTarget.isName()) {
875 |             return true;
876 |           }
877 | 
878 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
879 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
880 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
881 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
882 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
883 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
884 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
885 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
886 |             return true;
887 |           }
888 | 
889 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
890 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
891 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
892 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
893 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
894 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
895 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
896 |               return false;
897 |             }
898 | 
899 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
900 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
901 |             // other objects.
902 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
903 |             // side-effect.
904 |             while (isGet(current)) {
905 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
906 |             }
907 | 
908 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
909 |           } else {
910 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
911 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
912 |             // preserve existing behavior.
913 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
914 |           }
915 |         }
916 | 
917 |         return true;
918 |     }
919 | 
920 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
921 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
922 |         return true;
923 |       }
924 |     }
925 | 
926 |     return false;
927 |   }

/**

   * These names will be the root of dependency trees.

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.
   *
   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the

===== M0483 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.defineDeclaredProperty(String,JSType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 271-283
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property whose type is synthesized (i.e. not inferred).
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the declaration of property
   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
271 |   public final boolean defineDeclaredProperty(String propertyName,
272 |       JSType type, Node propertyNode) {
273 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, false,
274 |         propertyNode);
275 | 
276 |     // All property definitions go through this method
277 |     // or defineDeclaredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
278 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
279 |     // to register this after defining the property.

===== M0484 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 136-157
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
136 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
137 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
138 |     String methodName = null;
139 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
140 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
141 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
142 |       String name = callName.getString();
143 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
144 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
145 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     if (methodName != null) {
150 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
151 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
152 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
153 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
154 |       }
155 |     }
156 |     return null;
157 |   }

===== M0489 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

708 |   private List<PassFactory> getCodeRemovingPasses() {

710 |     if (options.collapseObjectLiterals && !isInliningForbidden()) {

Documentation:
/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
595 |   public Scope getScope() {
596 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
597 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
598 |       return scope;
599 |     }

605 |     }
606 |     scopeRoots.clear();
607 | 
608 |     return scope;
609 |   }

===== M0512 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CompilerOptions.getReplacementsHelper(Map<String, Object>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CompilerOptions.java
Original range: 917-935
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a map of String->Node from a map of String->Number/String/Boolean.
   */
Method code:
917 |   private static Map<String, Node> getReplacementsHelper(
918 |       Map<String, Object> source) {
919 |     Map<String, Node> map = Maps.newHashMap();
920 |     for (Map.Entry<String, Object> entry : source.entrySet()) {
921 |       String name = entry.getKey();
922 |       Object value = entry.getValue();
923 |       if (value instanceof Boolean) {
924 |         map.put(name, NodeUtil.booleanNode(((Boolean) value).booleanValue()));
925 |       } else if (value instanceof Integer) {
926 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Integer) value).intValue()));
927 |       } else if (value instanceof Double) {
928 |         map.put(name, IR.number(((Double) value).doubleValue()));
929 |       } else {
930 |         Preconditions.checkState(value instanceof String);
931 |         map.put(name, IR.string((String) value));
932 |       }
933 |     }
934 |     return map;
935 |   }

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1902-1904
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1902 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1903 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1904 |   }

844 |       }
845 |     }
846 |   }

===== M0539 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1177-1185
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1177 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1178 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1179 |       return -1;
1180 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1181 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1182 |     } else {
1183 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1184 |     }
1185 |   }

87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
