===== M0558 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindDependencyScopes.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 508-551
Documentation:

Method code:
508 |     @Override
509 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
510 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope()) {
511 |         return;
512 |       }
513 | 
514 |       if (n.isAssign()) {
515 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
516 |         NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, nameNode, n);
517 |         if (ns != null) {
518 |           if (parent.isFor() && !NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
519 |             // Patch for assignments that appear in the init,
520 |             // condition or iteration part of a FOR loop.  Without
521 |             // this change, all 3 of those parts try to claim the for
522 |             // loop as their dependency scope.  The last assignment in
523 |             // those three fields wins, which can result in incorrect
524 |             // reference edges between referenced and assigned variables.
525 |             //
526 |             // TODO(user) revisit the dependency scope calculation
527 |             // logic.
528 |             if (parent.getFirstChild().getNext() != n) {
529 |               recordDepScope(n, ns);
530 |             } else {
531 |               recordDepScope(nameNode, ns);
532 |             }
533 |           } else {
534 |             recordDepScope(n, ns);
535 |           }
536 |         }
537 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
538 |         NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, n, parent);
539 |         recordDepScope(n, ns);
540 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
541 |         NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, n.getFirstChild(), n);
542 |         recordDepScope(n, ns);
543 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isExprCall(n)) {
544 |         Node callNode = n.getFirstChild();
545 |         Node nameNode = callNode.getFirstChild();
546 |         NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, nameNode, callNode);
547 |         if (ns != null && ns.onlyAffectsClassDef) {
548 |           recordDepScope(n, ns);
549 |         }
550 |       }
551 |     }

===== M0566 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2893-2957
Documentation:
/**
   * @param locals A predicate to apply to unknown local values.
   * @return Whether the node is known to be a value that is not a reference
   *     outside the expression scope.
   */
Method code:
2893 |   static boolean evaluatesToLocalValue(Node value, Predicate<Node> locals) {
2894 |     switch (value.getType()) {
2895 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
2896 |         // A result that is aliased by a non-local name, is the effectively the
2897 |         // same as returning a non-local name, but this doesn't matter if the
2898 |         // value is immutable.
2899 |         return NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(value.getLastChild())
2900 |             || (locals.apply(value)
2901 |                 && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals));
2902 |       case Token.COMMA:
2903 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2904 |       case Token.AND:
2905 |       case Token.OR:
2906 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals)
2907 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2908 |       case Token.HOOK:
2909 |         return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild().getNext(), locals)
2910 |            && evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getLastChild(), locals);
2911 |       case Token.INC:
2912 |       case Token.DEC:
2913 |         if (value.getBooleanProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP)) {
2914 |           return evaluatesToLocalValue(value.getFirstChild(), locals);
2915 |         } else {
2916 |           return true;
2917 |         }
2918 |       case Token.THIS:
2919 |         return locals.apply(value);
2920 |       case Token.NAME:
2921 |         return isImmutableValue(value) || locals.apply(value);
2922 |       case Token.GETELEM:
2923 |       case Token.GETPROP:
2924 |         // There is no information about the locality of object properties.
2925 |         return locals.apply(value);
2926 |       case Token.CALL:
2927 |         return callHasLocalResult(value)
2928 |             || isToStringMethodCall(value)
2929 |             || locals.apply(value);
2930 |       case Token.NEW:
2931 |         return newHasLocalResult(value)
2932 |                || locals.apply(value);
2933 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
2934 |       case Token.REGEXP:
2935 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
2936 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
2937 |         // Literals objects with non-literal children are allowed.
2938 |         return true;
2939 |       case Token.DELPROP:
2940 |       case Token.IN:
2941 |         // TODO(johnlenz): should IN operator be included in #isSimpleOperator?
2942 |         return true;
2943 |       default:
2944 |         // Other op force a local value:
2945 |         //  x = '' + g (x is now an local string)
2946 |         //  x -= g (x is now an local number)
2947 |         if (isAssignmentOp(value)
2948 |             || isSimpleOperator(value)
2949 |             || isImmutableValue(value)) {
2950 |           return true;
2951 |         }
2952 | 
2953 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
2954 |             "Unexpected expression node" + value +
2955 |             "\n parent:" + value.getParent());
2956 |     }
2957 |   }

===== M0574 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 37-65
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
37 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
38 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
39 |     // can be simplified.
40 | 
41 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
42 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
43 |     if (parent == null) {
44 |       return null;
45 |     }
46 | 
47 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
48 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
49 |     } else if (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
50 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
51 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
52 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
53 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
54 |       }
55 |     } else if (parent.isAssign() && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
56 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
57 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
58 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
59 |           isExtern);
60 |     } else if (parent.isParamList()) {
61 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
62 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
63 |     }
64 |     return null;
65 |   }

===== M0575 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 51-93
Documentation:

Method code:
51 |   @Override
52 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
53 |     Node node = error.node;
54 |     if (node != null) {
55 |       for (Node current = node;
56 |            current != null;
57 |            current = current.getParent()) {
58 |         int type = current.getType();
59 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
60 | 
61 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
62 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
63 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
64 |         // a bad idea.
65 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
66 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(current);
67 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
68 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
69 |         } else if (type == Token.ASSIGN) {
70 |           Node rhs = current.getLastChild();
71 |           if (rhs.isFunction()) {
72 |             info = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(rhs);
73 |           }
74 |         }
75 | 
76 |         if (info != null) {
77 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
78 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
79 | 
80 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
81 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
82 |             if (guard != null) {
83 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
84 |               if (newLevel != null) {
85 |                 return newLevel;
86 |               }
87 |             }
88 |           }
89 |         }
90 |       }
91 |     }
92 |     return null;
93 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0605 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.referenceParentNames()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1287-1309
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds mutual references between all known global names and their parent
   * names. (e.g. between <code>a.b.c</code> and <code>a.b</code>).
   */
Method code:
1287 |   private void referenceParentNames() {
1288 |     // Duplicate set of nodes to process so we don't modify set we are
1289 |     // currently iterating over
1290 |     Set<JsName> allNamesCopy = Sets.newHashSet(allNames.values());
1291 | 
1292 |     for (JsName name : allNamesCopy) {
1293 |       String curName = name.name;
1294 |       JsName curJsName = name;
1295 |       while (curName.indexOf('.') != -1) {
1296 |         String parentName = curName.substring(0, curName.lastIndexOf('.'));
1297 |         if (!globalNames.contains(parentName)) {
1298 | 
1299 |           JsName parentJsName = getName(parentName, true);
1300 | 
1301 |           recordReference(curJsName.name, parentJsName.name, RefType.REGULAR);
1302 |           recordReference(parentJsName.name, curJsName.name, RefType.REGULAR);
1303 | 
1304 |           curJsName = parentJsName;
1305 |         }
1306 |         curName = parentName;
1307 |       }
1308 |     }
1309 |   }

498 |   Var declare(String name, Node nameNode,
499 |       JSType type, CompilerInput input, boolean inferred) {

189 |   @Override
190 |   protected List<PassFactory> getChecks() {
191 |     List<PassFactory> checks = Lists.newArrayList();
192 | 
193 |     if (options.closurePass) {
194 |       checks.add(closureGoogScopeAliases);
195 |     }
196 | 
197 |     if (options.nameAnonymousFunctionsOnly) {
198 |       if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
199 |           AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.MAPPED) {
200 |         checks.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
201 |       } else if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
202 |           AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
203 |         checks.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
204 |       }
205 |       return checks;
206 |     }
207 | 
208 |     checks.add(checkSideEffects);
209 | 
210 |     if (options.checkSuspiciousCode ||
211 |         options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS) ||
212 |         options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.DEBUGGER_STATEMENT_PRESENT)) {
213 |       checks.add(suspiciousCode);

217 |         || options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT))  {
218 |       checks.add(checkControlStructures);
219 |     }
220 | 
221 |     if (options.checkRequires.isOn()) {
222 |       checks.add(checkRequires);
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     if (options.checkProvides.isOn()) {
226 |       checks.add(checkProvides);
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // The following passes are more like "preprocessor" passes.
230 |     // It's important that they run before most checking passes.
231 |     // Perhaps this method should be renamed?
232 |     if (options.generateExports) {
233 |       checks.add(generateExports);
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     if (options.exportTestFunctions) {
237 |       checks.add(exportTestFunctions);
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     if (options.closurePass) {
241 |       checks.add(closurePrimitives.makeOneTimePass());
242 |     }
243 | 
244 |     if (options.jqueryPass) {
245 |       checks.add(jqueryAliases.makeOneTimePass());
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     if (options.closurePass && options.checkMissingGetCssNameLevel.isOn()) {
249 |       checks.add(closureCheckGetCssName);
250 |     }
251 | 
252 |     if (options.syntheticBlockStartMarker != null) {
253 |       // This pass must run before the first fold constants pass.
254 |       checks.add(createSyntheticBlocks);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     checks.add(checkVars);
258 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
259 |       checks.add(checkRegExp);
260 |     }
261 | 
262 |     if (options.aggressiveVarCheck.isOn()) {
263 |       checks.add(checkVariableReferences);
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // This pass should run before types are assigned.
267 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
268 |       checks.add(objectPropertyStringPreprocess);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     if (options.checkTypes || options.inferTypes) {
272 |       checks.add(resolveTypes.makeOneTimePass());
273 |       checks.add(inferTypes.makeOneTimePass());
274 |       if (options.checkTypes) {
275 |         checks.add(checkTypes.makeOneTimePass());
276 |       } else {
277 |         checks.add(inferJsDocInfo.makeOneTimePass());
278 |       }
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     if (options.checkUnreachableCode.isOn() ||
282 |         (options.checkTypes && options.checkMissingReturn.isOn())) {
283 |       checks.add(checkControlFlow);
284 |     }
285 | 
286 |     // CheckAccessControls only works if check types is on.
287 |     if (options.checkTypes &&
288 |         (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.ACCESS_CONTROLS)
289 |          || options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CONSTANT_PROPERTY))) {
290 |       checks.add(checkAccessControls);
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     if (options.checkGlobalNamesLevel.isOn()) {
294 |       checks.add(checkGlobalNames);
295 |     }
296 | 
297 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT) || options.checkCaja) {
298 |       checks.add(checkStrictMode);
299 |     }
300 | 
301 |     // Replace 'goog.getCssName' before processing defines but after the
302 |     // other checks have been done.
303 |     if (options.closurePass) {
304 |       checks.add(closureReplaceGetCssName);
305 |     }
306 | 
307 |     // i18n
308 |     // If you want to customize the compiler to use a different i18n pass,
309 |     // you can create a PassConfig that calls replacePassFactory
310 |     // to replace this.
311 |     checks.add(options.messageBundle != null ?
312 |         replaceMessages : createEmptyPass("replaceMessages"));
313 | 
314 |     if (options.getTweakProcessing().isOn()) {
315 |       checks.add(processTweaks);
316 |     }
317 | 
318 |     // Defines in code always need to be processed.
319 |     checks.add(processDefines);
320 | 
321 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null ||
322 |         options.recordFunctionInformation) {
323 |       checks.add(computeFunctionNames);
324 |     }
325 | 
326 |     if (options.nameReferenceGraphPath != null &&
327 |         !options.nameReferenceGraphPath.isEmpty()) {
328 |       checks.add(printNameReferenceGraph);
329 |     }
330 | 
331 |     if (options.nameReferenceReportPath != null &&
332 |         !options.nameReferenceReportPath.isEmpty()) {
333 |       checks.add(printNameReferenceReport);
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(checks);
337 |     return checks;
338 |   }

/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
67 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
68 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
69 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
70 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1216 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1217 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1218 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1219 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1220 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1221 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1222 |       } else {
1223 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1224 |       }
1225 |     }
1226 |     return builder.build();
1227 |   }

===== M0662 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2318-2334
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2318 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2319 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2320 |     int c;
2321 |     while (true) {
2322 |       c = stream.getChar();
2323 |       if (c == ' ') {
2324 |         continue;
2325 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2326 |         matchedLc = true;
2327 |         break;
2328 |       } else {
2329 |         break;
2330 |       }
2331 |     }
2332 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2333 |     return matchedLc;
2334 |   }

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }

251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;

258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

===== M0670 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ProcessDefines.CollectDefines.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ProcessDefines.java
Original range: 286-371
Documentation:

Method code:
286 |     @Override
287 |     public  void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
288 |       RefInfo refInfo = allRefInfo.get(n);
289 |       if (refInfo != null) {
290 |         Ref ref = refInfo.ref;
291 |         Name name = refInfo.name;
292 |         String fullName = name.getFullName();
293 |         switch (ref.type) {
294 |           case SET_FROM_GLOBAL:
295 |           case SET_FROM_LOCAL:
296 |             Node valParent = getValueParent(ref);
297 |             Node val = valParent.getLastChild();
298 |             if (valParent.isAssign() && name.isSimpleName() &&
299 |                 name.getDeclaration() == ref) {
300 |               // For defines, it's an error if a simple name is assigned
301 |               // before it's declared
302 |               compiler.report(
303 |                   t.makeError(val, INVALID_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, fullName));
304 |             } else if (processDefineAssignment(t, fullName, val, valParent)) {
305 |               // remove the assignment so that the variable is still declared,
306 |               // but no longer assigned to a value, e.g.,
307 |               // DEF_FOO = 5; // becomes "5;"
308 | 
309 |               // We can't remove the ASSIGN/VAR when we're still visiting its
310 |               // children, so we'll have to come back later to remove it.
311 |               refInfo.name.removeRef(ref);
312 |               lvalueToRemoveLater = valParent;
313 |             }
314 |             break;
315 |           default:
316 |             if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
317 |               // Treat this as a reference to a define in the global scope.
318 |               // After this point, the define must not be reassigned,
319 |               // or it's an error.
320 |               DefineInfo info = assignableDefines.get(fullName);
321 |               if (info != null) {
322 |                 setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
323 |                 assignableDefines.remove(fullName);
324 |               }
325 |             }
326 |             break;
327 |         }
328 |       }
329 | 
330 |       if (!t.inGlobalScope() &&
331 |           n.getJSDocInfo() != null && n.getJSDocInfo().isDefine()) {
332 |         // warn about @define annotations in local scopes
333 |         compiler.report(
334 |             t.makeError(n, NON_GLOBAL_DEFINE_INIT_ERROR, ""));
335 |       }
336 | 
337 |       if (lvalueToRemoveLater == n) {
338 |         lvalueToRemoveLater = null;
339 |         if (n.isAssign()) {
340 |           Node last = n.getLastChild();
341 |           n.removeChild(last);
342 |           parent.replaceChild(n, last);
343 |         } else {
344 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.isName());
345 |           n.removeChild(n.getFirstChild());
346 |         }
347 |         compiler.reportCodeChange();
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       if (n.isCall()) {
351 |         if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
352 |           // If there's a function call in the global scope,
353 |           // we just say it's unsafe and freeze all the defines.
354 |           //
355 |           // NOTE(nicksantos): We could be a lot smarter here. For example,
356 |           // ReplaceOverriddenVars keeps a call graph of all functions and
357 |           // which functions/variables that they reference, and tries
358 |           // to statically determine which functions are "safe" and which
359 |           // are not. But this would be overkill, expecially because
360 |           // the intended use of defines is with config_files, where
361 |           // all the defines are at the top of the bundle.
362 |           for (DefineInfo info : assignableDefines.values()) {
363 |             setDefineInfoNotAssignable(info, t);
364 |           }
365 | 
366 |           assignableDefines.clear();
367 |         }
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       updateAssignAllowedStack(n, false);
371 |     }

===== M0672 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3003-3029
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3003 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3004 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3005 |     if (info == null) {
3006 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3007 |       if (parent == null) {
3008 |         return null;
3009 |       }
3010 | 
3011 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3012 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3013 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3014 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3015 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3016 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3017 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3018 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3019 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3020 |         return parent.getJSDocInfo();
3021 |       } else if ((parent.isHook() && parent.getFirstChild() != n) ||
3022 |                  parent.isOr() ||
3023 |                  parent.isAnd() ||
3024 |                  (parent.isComma() && parent.getFirstChild() != n)) {
3025 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3026 |       }
3027 |     }
3028 |     return info;
3029 |   }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.runCallable(Callable<T>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 591-643
Documentation:

Method code:
591 |   @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
592 |   static <T> T runCallable(
593 |       final Callable<T> callable, boolean useLargeStackThread, boolean trace) {
594 | 
595 |     // Under JRE 1.6, the jscompiler overflows the stack when running on some
596 |     // large or complex js code. Here we start a new thread with a larger
597 |     // stack in order to let the compiler do its thing, without having to
598 |     // increase the stack size for *every* thread (which is what -Xss does).
599 |     // Might want to add thread pool support for clients that compile a lot.
600 | 
601 |     final boolean dumpTraceReport = trace;
602 |     final Object[] result = new Object[1];
603 |     final Throwable[] exception = new Throwable[1];
604 |     Runnable runnable = new Runnable() {
605 |       @Override
606 |       public void run() {
607 |         try {
608 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
609 |             Tracer.initCurrentThreadTrace();
610 |           }
611 |           result[0] = callable.call();
612 |         } catch (Throwable e) {
613 |           exception[0] = e;
614 |         } finally {
615 |           if (dumpTraceReport) {
616 |             Tracer.logAndClearCurrentThreadTrace();
617 |           }
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 |     };
621 | 
622 |     if (useLargeStackThread) {
623 |       Thread th = new Thread(null, runnable, "jscompiler", COMPILER_STACK_SIZE);
624 |       th.start();
625 |       while (true) {
626 |         try {
627 |           th.join();
628 |           break;
629 |         } catch (InterruptedException ignore) {
630 |           // ignore
631 |         }
632 |       }
633 |     } else {
634 |       runnable.run();
635 |     }
636 | 
637 |     // Pass on any exception caught by the runnable object.
638 |     if (exception[0] != null) {
639 |       throw new RuntimeException(exception[0]);
640 |     }
641 | 
642 |     return (T) result[0];
643 |   }

===== M0689 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 575-607
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
575 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
576 |       Preconditions.checkState(
577 |           functionBody.getParent().isFunction());
578 |       Node previous = null;
579 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
580 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
581 |       // are already in the right place.
582 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
583 |         previous = current;
584 |         current = current.getNext();
585 |       }
586 | 
587 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
588 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
589 |       while (current != null) {
590 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
591 |         Node next = current.getNext();
592 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
593 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
594 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
595 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
596 | 
597 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
598 |           // previous declarations).
599 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
600 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
601 |         } else {
602 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
603 |           previous = current;
604 |         }
605 |         current = next;
606 |       }
607 |     }

===== M0711 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefaultPassConfig.getOptimizations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefaultPassConfig.java
Original range: 340-673
Documentation:

Method code:
340 |   @Override
341 |   protected List<PassFactory> getOptimizations() {
342 |     List<PassFactory> passes = Lists.newArrayList();
343 |     passes.add(garbageCollectChecks);
344 | 
345 |     // TODO(nicksantos): The order of these passes makes no sense, and needs
346 |     // to be re-arranged.
347 | 
348 |     if (options.runtimeTypeCheck) {
349 |       passes.add(runtimeTypeCheck);
350 |     }
351 | 
352 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeStandardOptimizations"));
353 | 
354 |     if (options.replaceIdGenerators) {
355 |       passes.add(replaceIdGenerators);
356 |     }
357 | 
358 |     // Optimizes references to the arguments variable.
359 |     if (options.optimizeArgumentsArray) {
360 |       passes.add(optimizeArgumentsArray);
361 |     }
362 | 
363 |     // Abstract method removal works best on minimally modified code, and also
364 |     // only needs to run once.
365 |     if (options.closurePass &&
366 |         (options.removeAbstractMethods || options.removeClosureAsserts)) {
367 |       passes.add(closureCodeRemoval);
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     // Collapsing properties can undo constant inlining, so we do this before
371 |     // the main optimization loop.
372 |     if (options.collapseProperties) {
373 |       passes.add(collapseProperties);
374 |     }
375 | 
376 |     // ReplaceStrings runs after CollapseProperties in order to simplify
377 |     // pulling in values of constants defined in enums structures.
378 |     if (!options.replaceStringsFunctionDescriptions.isEmpty()) {
379 |       passes.add(replaceStrings);
380 |     }
381 | 
382 |     // Tighten types based on actual usage.
383 |     if (options.tightenTypes) {
384 |       passes.add(tightenTypesBuilder);
385 |     }
386 | 
387 |     // Property disambiguation should only run once and needs to be done
388 |     // soon after type checking, both so that it can make use of type
389 |     // information and so that other passes can take advantage of the renamed
390 |     // properties.
391 |     if (options.disambiguateProperties) {
392 |       passes.add(disambiguateProperties);
393 |     }
394 | 
395 |     if (options.computeFunctionSideEffects) {
396 |       passes.add(markPureFunctions);
397 |     } else if (options.markNoSideEffectCalls) {
398 |       // TODO(user) The properties that this pass adds to CALL and NEW
399 |       // AST nodes increase the AST's in-memory size.  Given that we are
400 |       // already running close to our memory limits, we could run into
401 |       // trouble if we end up using the @nosideeffects annotation a lot
402 |       // or compute @nosideeffects annotations by looking at function
403 |       // bodies.  It should be easy to propagate @nosideeffects
404 |       // annotations as part of passes that depend on this property and
405 |       // store the result outside the AST (which would allow garbage
406 |       // collection once the pass is done).
407 |       passes.add(markNoSideEffectCalls);
408 |     }
409 | 
410 |     if (options.chainCalls) {
411 |       passes.add(chainCalls);
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Constant checking must be done after property collapsing because
415 |     // property collapsing can introduce new constants (e.g. enum values).
416 |     if (options.inlineConstantVars) {
417 |       passes.add(checkConsts);
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     // The Caja library adds properties to Object.prototype, which breaks
421 |     // most for-in loops.  This adds a check to each loop that skips
422 |     // any property matching /___$/.
423 |     if (options.ignoreCajaProperties) {
424 |       passes.add(ignoreCajaProperties);
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     assertAllOneTimePasses(passes);
428 | 
429 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval || options.reportPath != null) {
430 |       passes.addAll(getCodeRemovingPasses());
431 |       passes.add(smartNamePass);
432 |     }
433 | 
434 |     // This needs to come after the inline constants pass, which is run within
435 |     // the code removing passes.
436 |     if (options.closurePass) {
437 |       passes.add(closureOptimizePrimitives);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // TODO(user): This forces a first crack at crossModuleCodeMotion
441 |     // before devirtualization. Once certain functions are devirtualized,
442 |     // it confuses crossModuleCodeMotion ability to recognized that
443 |     // it is recursive.
444 | 
445 |     // TODO(user): This is meant for a temporary quick win.
446 |     // In the future, we might want to improve our analysis in
447 |     // CrossModuleCodeMotion so we don't need to do this.
448 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
449 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
450 |     }
451 | 
452 |     // Method devirtualization benefits from property disambiguiation so
453 |     // it should run after that pass but before passes that do
454 |     // optimizations based on global names (like cross module code motion
455 |     // and inline functions).  Smart Name Removal does better if run before
456 |     // this pass.
457 |     if (options.devirtualizePrototypeMethods) {
458 |       passes.add(devirtualizePrototypeMethods);
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
462 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
463 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.BEFORE_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
464 |     }
465 | 
466 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeMainOptimizations"));
467 | 
468 |     if (options.specializeInitialModule) {
469 |       // When specializing the initial module, we want our fixups to be
470 |       // as lean as possible, so we run the entire optimization loop to a
471 |       // fixed point before specializing, then specialize, and then run the
472 |       // main optimization loop again.
473 | 
474 |       passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
475 | 
476 |       if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
477 |         passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
478 |       }
479 | 
480 |       if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
481 |         passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
482 |       }
483 | 
484 |       passes.add(specializeInitialModule.makeOneTimePass());
485 |     }
486 | 
487 |     passes.addAll(getMainOptimizationLoop());
488 | 
489 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("beforeModuleMotion"));
490 | 
491 |     if (options.crossModuleCodeMotion) {
492 |       passes.add(crossModuleCodeMotion);
493 |     }
494 | 
495 |     if (options.crossModuleMethodMotion) {
496 |       passes.add(crossModuleMethodMotion);
497 |     }
498 | 
499 |     passes.add(createEmptyPass("afterModuleMotion"));
500 | 
501 |     // Some optimizations belong outside the loop because running them more
502 |     // than once would either have no benefit or be incorrect.
503 |     if (options.customPasses != null) {
504 |       passes.add(getCustomPasses(
505 |           CustomPassExecutionTime.AFTER_OPTIMIZATION_LOOP));
506 |     }
507 | 
508 |     if (options.flowSensitiveInlineVariables) {
509 |       passes.add(flowSensitiveInlineVariables);
510 | 
511 |       // After inlining some of the variable uses, some variables are unused.
512 |       // Re-run remove unused vars to clean it up.
513 |       if (options.removeUnusedVars || options.removeUnusedLocalVars) {
514 |         passes.add(removeUnusedVars);
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 | 
518 |     // Running this pass again is required to have goog.events compile down to
519 |     // nothing when compiled on its own.
520 |     if (options.smartNameRemoval) {
521 |       passes.add(smartNamePass2);
522 |     }
523 | 
524 |     if (options.collapseAnonymousFunctions) {
525 |       passes.add(collapseAnonymousFunctions);
526 |     }
527 | 
528 |     // Move functions before extracting prototype member declarations.
529 |     if (options.moveFunctionDeclarations ||
530 |         // renamePrefixNamescape relies on moveFunctionDeclarations
531 |         // to preserve semantics.
532 |         options.renamePrefixNamespace != null) {
533 |       passes.add(moveFunctionDeclarations);
534 |     }
535 | 
536 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
537 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.MAPPED) {
538 |       passes.add(nameMappedAnonymousFunctions);
539 |     }
540 | 
541 |     // The mapped name anonymous function pass makes use of information that
542 |     // the extract prototype member declarations pass removes so the former
543 |     // happens before the latter.
544 |     //
545 |     // Extracting prototype properties screws up the heuristic renaming
546 |     // policies, so never run it when those policies are requested.
547 |     if (options.extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations &&
548 |         (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.HEURISTIC &&
549 |          options.propertyRenaming !=
550 |             PropertyRenamingPolicy.AGGRESSIVE_HEURISTIC)) {
551 |       passes.add(extractPrototypeMemberDeclarations);
552 |     }
553 | 
554 |     if (options.ambiguateProperties &&
555 |         (options.propertyRenaming == PropertyRenamingPolicy.ALL_UNQUOTED)) {
556 |       passes.add(ambiguateProperties);
557 |     }
558 | 
559 |     if (options.propertyRenaming != PropertyRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
560 |       passes.add(renameProperties);
561 |     }
562 | 
563 |     // Reserve global names added to the "windows" object.
564 |     if (options.reserveRawExports) {
565 |       passes.add(gatherRawExports);
566 |     }
567 | 
568 |     // This comes after property renaming because quoted property names must
569 |     // not be renamed.
570 |     if (options.convertToDottedProperties) {
571 |       passes.add(convertToDottedProperties);
572 |     }
573 | 
574 |     // Property renaming must happen before this pass runs since this
575 |     // pass may convert dotted properties into quoted properties.  It
576 |     // is beneficial to run before alias strings, alias keywords and
577 |     // variable renaming.
578 |     if (options.rewriteFunctionExpressions) {
579 |       passes.add(rewriteFunctionExpressions);
580 |     }
581 | 
582 |     // This comes after converting quoted property accesses to dotted property
583 |     // accesses in order to avoid aliasing property names.
584 |     if (!options.aliasableStrings.isEmpty() || options.aliasAllStrings) {
585 |       passes.add(aliasStrings);
586 |     }
587 | 
588 |     if (options.aliasExternals) {
589 |       passes.add(aliasExternals);
590 |     }
591 | 
592 |     if (options.aliasKeywords) {
593 |       passes.add(aliasKeywords);
594 |     }
595 | 
596 |     // Passes after this point can no longer depend on normalized AST
597 |     // assumptions.
598 |     passes.add(markUnnormalized);
599 | 
600 |     if (options.coalesceVariableNames) {
601 |       passes.add(coalesceVariableNames);
602 | 
603 |       // coalesceVariables creates identity assignments and more redundant code
604 |       // that can be removed, rerun the peephole optimizations to clean them
605 |       // up.
606 |       if (options.foldConstants) {
607 |         passes.add(peepholeOptimizations);
608 |       }
609 |     }
610 | 
611 |     if (options.collapseVariableDeclarations) {
612 |       passes.add(exploitAssign);
613 |       passes.add(collapseVariableDeclarations);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     // This pass works best after collapseVariableDeclarations.
617 |     passes.add(denormalize);
618 | 
619 |     if (options.instrumentationTemplate != null) {
620 |       passes.add(instrumentFunctions);
621 |     }
622 | 
623 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.ALL) {
624 |       // If we're leaving some (or all) variables with their old names,
625 |       // then we need to undo any of the markers we added for distinguishing
626 |       // local variables ("$$1").
627 |       passes.add(invertContextualRenaming);
628 |     }
629 | 
630 |     if (options.variableRenaming != VariableRenamingPolicy.OFF) {
631 |       passes.add(renameVars);
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     if (options.groupVariableDeclarations) {
635 |       passes.add(groupVariableDeclarations);
636 |     }
637 | 
638 |     // This pass should run after names stop changing.
639 |     if (options.processObjectPropertyString) {
640 |       passes.add(objectPropertyStringPostprocess);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     if (options.labelRenaming) {
644 |       passes.add(renameLabels);
645 |     }
646 | 
647 |     if (options.foldConstants) {
648 |       passes.add(latePeepholeOptimizations);
649 |     }
650 | 
651 |     if (options.anonymousFunctionNaming ==
652 |         AnonymousFunctionNamingPolicy.UNMAPPED) {
653 |       passes.add(nameUnmappedAnonymousFunctions);
654 |     }
655 | 
656 |     if (options.renamePrefixNamespace != null) {
657 |       if (!GLOBAL_SYMBOL_NAMESPACE_PATTERN.matcher(

659 |         throw new IllegalArgumentException(

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1200-1350
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1200 |   Node parseInputs() {
1201 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1202 | 
1203 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1204 |     // individual file parse trees.
1205 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1206 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1207 |     }
1208 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1209 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1210 |     }
1211 | 
1212 |     // Parse main js sources.
1213 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1214 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1215 | 
1216 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1217 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1218 | 
1219 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1220 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1221 | 
1222 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1223 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1224 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1225 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1226 |     }
1227 | 
1228 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1229 | 
1230 |     try {
1231 |       // Parse externs sources.
1232 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1233 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1234 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1235 |           return null;
1236 |         }
1237 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1238 |       }
1239 | 
1240 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1241 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1242 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1243 |       }
1244 | 
1245 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1246 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1247 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1248 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1249 | 
1250 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1251 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1252 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1253 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1254 |           }
1255 |         }
1256 | 
1257 |         try {
1258 |           inputs =
1259 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1260 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1261 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1262 |           report(JSError.make(
1263 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1264 | 
1265 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1266 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1267 |             return null;
1268 |           }
1269 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1270 |           report(JSError.make(
1271 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1272 | 
1273 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1274 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1275 |             return null;
1276 |           }
1277 |         }
1278 |       }
1279 | 
1280 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1281 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1282 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1283 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1284 | 
1285 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1286 |         if (n == null) {
1287 |           continue;
1288 |         }
1289 | 
1290 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1291 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1292 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1293 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1294 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1295 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1296 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1297 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1298 | 
1299 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1300 | 
1301 |             externs.add(input);
1302 |             staleInputs = true;
1303 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1304 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1305 |             staleInputs = true;
1306 |           }
1307 |         }
1308 |       }
1309 | 
1310 |       if (staleInputs) {
1311 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1312 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1313 |       }
1314 | 
1315 |       // Build the AST.
1316 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1317 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1318 |         if (n == null) {
1319 |           continue;
1320 |         }
1321 | 
1322 |         if (devMode) {
1323 |           runSanityCheck();
1324 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1325 |             return null;
1326 |           }
1327 |         }
1328 | 
1329 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1330 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1331 | 
1332 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1333 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1334 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1335 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1336 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1337 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1338 |         }
1339 | 
1340 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1341 |       }
1342 | 
1343 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1344 |         return null;
1345 |       }
1346 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1347 |     } finally {
1348 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1349 |     }
1350 |   }

/**
     * A value whose result is the return value of a function call
     * can be an alias to global object. The dependency on the call target will
     * prevent the removal of the function and its dependent values, but won't
     * prevent the alias' removal.
     */
Method code:
934 |     private boolean maybeHiddenAlias(String name, Node n) {
935 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
936 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, parent)) {
937 |         Node rhs = (parent.isVar())
938 |             ? n.getFirstChild() : parent.getLastChild();
939 |         return (rhs != null && !NodeUtil.evaluatesToLocalValue(

942 |       return false;
943 |     }

===== M0719 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CheckSideEffects.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CheckSideEffects.java
Original range: 83-166
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   @Override
84 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
85 |     // VOID nodes appear when there are extra semicolons at the BLOCK level.
86 |     // I've been unable to think of any cases where this indicates a bug,
87 |     // and apparently some people like keeping these semicolons around,
88 |     // so we'll allow it.
89 |     if (n.isEmpty() ||
90 |         n.isComma()) {
91 |       return;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     if (parent == null) {
95 |       return;
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     int pt = parent.getType();
99 |     if (pt == Token.COMMA) {
100 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
101 |       if (gramps.isCall() &&
102 |           parent == gramps.getFirstChild()) {
103 |         // Semantically, a direct call to eval is different from an indirect
104 |         // call to an eval. See Ecma-262 S15.1.2.1. So it's ok for the first
105 |         // expression to a comma to be a no-op if it's used to indirect
106 |         // an eval.
107 |         if (n == parent.getFirstChild() &&
108 |             parent.getChildCount() == 2 &&
109 |             n.getNext().isName() &&
110 |             "eval".equals(n.getNext().getString())) {
111 |           return;
112 |         }
113 |       }
114 | 
115 |       if (n == parent.getLastChild()) {
116 |         for (Node an : parent.getAncestors()) {
117 |           int ancestorType = an.getType();
118 |           if (ancestorType == Token.COMMA)
119 |             continue;
120 |           if (ancestorType != Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
121 |               ancestorType != Token.BLOCK)
122 |             return;
123 |           else
124 |             break;
125 |         }
126 |       }
127 |     } else if (pt != Token.EXPR_RESULT && pt != Token.BLOCK) {
128 |       if (pt == Token.FOR && parent.getChildCount() == 4 &&
129 |           (n == parent.getFirstChild() ||
130 |            n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext())) {
131 |         // Fall through and look for warnings for the 1st and 3rd child
132 |         // of a for.
133 |       } else {
134 |         return;  // it might be ok to not have a side-effect
135 |       }
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     boolean isSimpleOp = NodeUtil.isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType());
139 |     if (isSimpleOp ||
140 |         !NodeUtil.mayHaveSideEffects(n, t.getCompiler())) {
141 |       if (n.isQualifiedName() && n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
142 |         // This no-op statement was there so that JSDoc information could
143 |         // be attached to the name. This check should not complain about it.
144 |         return;
145 |       } else if (n.isExprResult()) {
146 |         // we already reported the problem when we visited the child.
147 |         return;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       String msg = "This code lacks side-effects. Is there a bug?";
151 |       if (n.isString()) {
152 |         msg = "Is there a missing '+' on the previous line?";
153 |       } else if (isSimpleOp) {
154 |         msg = "The result of the '" + Token.name(n.getType()).toLowerCase() +
155 |             "' operator is not being used.";
156 |       }
157 | 
158 |       t.getCompiler().report(
159 |           t.makeError(n, level, USELESS_CODE_ERROR, msg));
160 |       // TODO(johnlenz): determine if it is necessary to
161 |       // try to protect side-effect free statements as well.
162 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
163 |         problemNodes.add(n);
164 |       }
165 |     }
166 |   }

149 |         break;

152 |     }

===== M0727 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.FixedPointGraphTraversal.computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E>,Set<N>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/graph/FixedPointGraphTraversal.java
Original range: 93-128
Documentation:
/**
   * Compute a fixed point for the given graph, entering from the given nodes.
   * @param graph The graph to traverse.
   * @param entrySet The nodes to begin traversing from.
   */
Method code:
93 |   public void computeFixedPoint(DiGraph<N, E> graph, Set<N> entrySet) {
94 |     int cycleCount = 0;
95 |     long nodeCount = graph.getNodes().size();
96 | 
97 |     // Choose a bail-out heuristically in case the computation
98 |     // doesn't converge.
99 |     long maxIterations = Math.max(nodeCount * nodeCount * nodeCount, 100);
100 | 
101 |     // Use a LinkedHashSet, so that the traversal is deterministic.
102 |     LinkedHashSet<DiGraphNode<N, E>> workSet =
103 |         Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
104 |     for (N n : entrySet) {
105 |       workSet.add(graph.getDirectedGraphNode(n));
106 |     }
107 |     for (; !workSet.isEmpty() && cycleCount < maxIterations; cycleCount++) {
108 |       // For every out edge in the workSet, traverse that edge. If that
109 |       // edge updates the state of the graph, then add the destination
110 |       // node to the resultSet, so that we can update all of its out edges
111 |       // on the next iteration.
112 |       DiGraphNode<N, E> source = workSet.iterator().next();
113 |       N sourceValue = source.getValue();
114 | 
115 |       workSet.remove(source);
116 | 
117 |       List<DiGraphEdge<N, E>> outEdges = source.getOutEdges();
118 |       for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> edge : outEdges) {
119 |         N destNode = edge.getDestination().getValue();
120 |         if (callback.traverseEdge(sourceValue, edge.getValue(), destNode)) {
121 |           workSet.add(edge.getDestination());
122 |         }
123 |       }
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(cycleCount != maxIterations,
127 |         NON_HALTING_ERROR_MSG);
128 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.JsNameRefNode.remove()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 310-336
Documentation:

Method code:
310 |     @Override
311 |     public void remove() {
312 |       // Setters have VAR, FUNCTION, or ASSIGN parent nodes. CALL parent
313 |       // nodes are global refs, and are handled later in this function.
314 |       Node containingNode = parent.getParent();
315 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
316 |         case Token.VAR:
317 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.hasOneChild());
318 |           replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
319 |           break;
320 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
321 |           replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
322 |           break;
323 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
324 |           if (containingNode.isExprResult()) {
325 |             replaceWithRhs(containingNode.getParent(), containingNode);
326 |           } else {
327 |             replaceWithRhs(containingNode, parent);
328 |           }
329 |           break;
330 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
331 |           // TODO(nicksantos): Come up with a way to remove this.
332 |           // If we remove object lit keys, then we will need to also
333 |           // create dependency scopes for them.
334 |           break;
335 |       }
336 |     }

===== M0739 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.collect(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 302-430
Documentation:

Method code:
302 |     public void collect(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
303 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
304 |         return;
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
308 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
309 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
310 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
311 |       }
312 | 
313 |       String name;
314 |       boolean isSet = false;
315 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
316 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
317 | 
318 |       switch (n.getType()) {
319 |         case Token.GETTER_DEF:
320 |         case Token.SETTER_DEF:
321 |         case Token.STRING:
322 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
323 |           name = null;
324 |           if (parent != null && parent.isObjectLit()) {
325 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
326 |           }
327 |           if (name == null) return;
328 |           isSet = true;
329 |           switch (n.getType()) {
330 |             case Token.STRING:
331 |               type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
332 |               break;
333 |             case Token.GETTER_DEF:
334 |               type = Name.Type.GET;
335 |               break;
336 |             case Token.SETTER_DEF:
337 |               type = Name.Type.SET;
338 |               break;
339 |             default:
340 |               throw new IllegalStateException("unexpected:" + n);
341 |           }
342 |           break;
343 |         case Token.NAME:
344 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
345 |           if (parent != null) {
346 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
347 |               case Token.VAR:
348 |                 isSet = true;
349 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
350 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
351 |                 break;
352 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
353 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
354 |                   isSet = true;
355 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
356 |                 }
357 |                 break;
358 |               case Token.GETPROP:
359 |                 return;
360 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
361 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
362 |                 if (gramps == null ||
363 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
364 |                 isSet = true;
365 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
366 |                 break;
367 |               case Token.INC:
368 |               case Token.DEC:
369 |                 isSet = true;
370 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
371 |                 break;
372 |               default:
373 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
374 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
375 |                   isSet = true;
376 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
377 |                 }
378 |             }
379 |           }
380 |           name = n.getString();
381 |           break;
382 |         case Token.GETPROP:
383 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
384 |           if (parent != null) {
385 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
386 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
387 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
388 |                   isSet = true;
389 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
390 |                   isPropAssign = true;
391 |                 }
392 |                 break;
393 |               case Token.INC:
394 |               case Token.DEC:
395 |                 isSet = true;
396 |                 type = Name.Type.OTHER;
397 |                 break;
398 |               case Token.GETPROP:
399 |                 return;
400 |               default:
401 |                 if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) &&
402 |                     parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
403 |                   isSet = true;
404 |                   type = Name.Type.OTHER;
405 |                 }
406 |             }
407 |           }
408 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
409 |           if (name == null) return;
410 |           break;
411 |         default:
412 |           return;
413 |       }
414 | 
415 |       // We are only interested in global names.
416 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
417 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
418 |         return;
419 |       }
420 | 
421 |       if (isSet) {
422 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
423 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
424 |         } else {
425 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
426 |         }
427 |       } else {
428 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
429 |       }
430 |     }

===== M0752 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.replaceWithRhs(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 1620-1646
Documentation:
/**
   * Replace n with a simpler expression, while preserving program
   * behavior.
   *
   * If the n's value is used, replace it with its rhs; otherwise
   * replace it with the subexpressions that have side effects.
   */
Method code:
1620 |   private void replaceWithRhs(Node parent, Node n) {
1621 |     if (valueConsumedByParent(n, parent)) {
1622 |       // parent reads from n directly; replace it with n's rhs + lhs
1623 |       // subexpressions with side effects.
1624 |       List<Node> replacements = getRhsSubexpressions(n);
1625 |       List<Node> newReplacements = Lists.newArrayList();
1626 |       for (int i = 0; i < replacements.size() - 1; i++) {
1627 |         newReplacements.addAll(getSideEffectNodes(replacements.get(i)));
1628 |       }
1629 |       Node valueExpr = replacements.get(replacements.size() - 1);
1630 |       valueExpr.detachFromParent();
1631 |       newReplacements.add(valueExpr);
1632 |       changeProxy.replaceWith(
1633 |           parent, n, collapseReplacements(newReplacements));
1634 |     } else if (n.isAssign() && !parent.isFor()) {
1635 |       // assignment appears in a RHS expression.  we have already
1636 |       // considered names in the assignment's RHS as being referenced;
1637 |       // replace the assignment with its RHS.
1638 |       // TODO(user) make the pass smarter about these cases and/or run
1639 |       // this pass and RemoveConstantExpressions together in a loop.
1640 |       Node replacement = n.getLastChild();
1641 |       replacement.detachFromParent();
1642 |       changeProxy.replaceWith(parent, n, replacement);
1643 |     } else {
1644 |       replaceTopLevelExpressionWithRhs(parent, n);
1645 |     }
1646 |   }

===== M0774 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 585-622
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is a literal value. We define a literal value
   * as any node that evaluates to the same thing regardless of when or
   * where it is evaluated. So /xyz/ and [3, 5] are literals, but
   * the name a is not.
   *
   * Function literals do not meet this definition, because they
   * lexically capture variables. For example, if you have
   * <code>
   * function() { return a; }
   * </code>
   * If it is evaluated in a different scope, then it
   * captures a different variable. Even if the function did not read
   * any captured vairables directly, it would still fail this definition,
   * because it affects the lifecycle of variables in the enclosing scope.
   *
   * However, a function literal with respect to a particular scope is
   * a literal.
   *
   * @param includeFunctions If true, all function expressions will be
   *     treated as literals.
   */
Method code:
585 |   static boolean isLiteralValue(Node n, boolean includeFunctions) {
586 |     switch (n.getType()) {
587 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
588 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
589 |              child = child.getNext()) {
590 |           if ((!child.isEmpty()) && !isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
591 |             return false;
592 |           }
593 |         }
594 |         return true;
595 | 
596 |       case Token.REGEXP:
597 |         // Return true only if all children are const.
598 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
599 |              child = child.getNext()) {
600 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child, includeFunctions)) {
601 |             return false;
602 |           }
603 |         }
604 |         return true;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
607 |         // Return true only if all values are const.
608 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
609 |              child = child.getNext()) {
610 |           if (!isLiteralValue(child.getFirstChild(), includeFunctions)) {
611 |             return false;
612 |           }
613 |         }
614 |         return true;
615 | 
616 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
617 |         return includeFunctions && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n);
618 | 
619 |       default:
620 |         return isImmutableValue(n);
621 |     }
622 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2399-2409
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2399 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2400 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2401 |     int len = s.length();
2402 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2403 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2404 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2405 |         return false;
2406 |       }
2407 |     }
2408 |     return true;
2409 |   }

===== M0799 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstChangeProxy.replaceWith(Node,Node,List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstChangeProxy.java
Original range: 94-131
Documentation:
/**
   * Replaces a node with the provided list.
   */
Method code:
94 |   final void replaceWith(Node parent, Node node, List<Node> replacements) {
95 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(replacements, "\"replacements\" is null.");
96 | 
97 |     int size = replacements.size();
98 | 
99 |     if ((size == 1) && node.isEquivalentTo(replacements.get(0))) {
100 |       // trees are equal... don't replace
101 |       return;
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     int parentType = parent.getType();
105 | 
106 |     Preconditions.checkState(size == 1 ||
107 |         parentType == Token.BLOCK ||
108 |         parentType == Token.SCRIPT ||
109 |         parentType == Token.LABEL);
110 | 
111 |     if (parentType == Token.LABEL && size != 1) {
112 |       Node block = IR.block();
113 |       for (Node newChild : replacements) {
114 |         newChild.copyInformationFrom(node);
115 |         Node oldParent = newChild.getParent();
116 |         block.addChildToBack(newChild);
117 |       }
118 | 
119 |       parent.replaceChild(node, block);
120 | 
121 |     } else {
122 |       for (Node newChild : replacements) {
123 |         newChild.copyInformationFrom(node);
124 |         Node oldParent = newChild.getParent();
125 |         parent.addChildBefore(newChild, node);
126 |       }
127 |       parent.removeChild(node);
128 | 
129 |     }
130 |     notifyOfRemoval(node);
131 |   }

===== M0800 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 476-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
476 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
477 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
478 |     int type = n.getType();
479 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
480 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
481 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
486 | 
487 |     switch (type) {
488 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
489 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       default:
493 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
494 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
495 |           // would no longer point to the true next
496 |           Node next = child.getNext();
497 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
498 |           child = next;
499 |         }
500 |         break;
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     curNode = n;
504 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
505 |   }

===== M0833 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 781-819
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
781 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
782 |         String name) {
783 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
784 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
785 |       // assignments.
786 | 
787 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
788 |       String prefix;
789 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
790 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
791 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
792 |       } else {
793 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
794 |         if (i == -1) {
795 |           return false;
796 |         }
797 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
798 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
799 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
800 |         while (i >= 0) {
801 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
802 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
803 |         }
804 |       }
805 | 
806 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
807 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
808 |         // key, so we're done.
809 |         return true;
810 |       }
811 | 
812 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
813 |         parent = n;
814 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
815 |       }
816 | 
817 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
818 |       return true;
819 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }
