===== M0858 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NameAnalyzer.FindReferences.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NameAnalyzer.java
Original range: 817-926
Documentation:

Method code:
817 |     @Override
818 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
819 |       if (!(n.isName() ||
820 |             NodeUtil.isGet(n) && !parent.isGetProp())) {
821 |         // This is not a simple or qualified name.
822 |         return;
823 |       }
824 | 
825 |       NameInformation nameInfo = createNameInformation(t, n, parent);
826 |       if (nameInfo == null) {
827 |         // The name is not a global name
828 |         return;
829 |       }
830 | 
831 |       if (nameInfo.onlyAffectsClassDef) {
832 |         if (nameInfo.superclass != null) {
833 |           recordReference(
834 |               nameInfo.name, nameInfo.superclass, RefType.INHERITANCE);
835 |         }
836 | 
837 |         // Make sure that we record a reference to the function that does
838 |         // the inheritance, so that the inherits() function itself does
839 |         // not get stripped.
840 |         String nodeName = n.getQualifiedName();
841 |         if (nodeName != null) {
842 |           recordReference(
843 |               nameInfo.name, nodeName, RefType.REGULAR);
844 |         }
845 | 
846 |         return;
847 |       }
848 | 
849 |       if (parent.isInstanceOf() &&
850 |           parent.getLastChild() == n &&
851 |           // Don't cover GETELEMs with a global root node.
852 |           n.isQualifiedName()) {
853 |         JsName checkedClass = getName(nameInfo.name, true);
854 |         refNodes.add(
855 |             new InstanceOfCheckNode(
856 |                 checkedClass, n, parent, parent.getParent()));
857 |         checkedClass.hasInstanceOfReference = true;
858 |         return;
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       // Determine which name might be potentially referring to this one by
862 |       // looking up the nearest enclosing dependency scope. It's unnecessary to
863 |       // determine all enclosing dependency scopes because this callback should
864 |       // create a chain of references between them.
865 |       NameInformation referring = getDependencyScope(n);
866 |       String referringName = "";
867 |       if (referring != null) {
868 |         referringName = referring.isPrototype
869 |                       ? referring.prototypeClass
870 |                       : referring.name;
871 |       }
872 | 
873 |       String name = nameInfo.name;
874 | 
875 |       // A value whose result is the return value of a function call
876 |       // can be an alias to global object.
877 |       // Here we add a alias to the general "global" object
878 |       // to act as a placeholder for the actual (unnamed) value.
879 |       if (maybeHiddenAlias(name, n)) {
880 |         recordAlias(name, WINDOW);
881 |       }
882 | 
883 |       // An externally referenceable name must always be defined, so we add a
884 |       // reference to it from the global scope (a.k.a. window).
885 |       if (nameInfo.isExternallyReferenceable) {
886 |         recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
887 |         maybeRecordAlias(name, parent, referring, referringName);
888 |         return;
889 |       }
890 | 
891 |       // An assignment implies a reference from the enclosing dependency scope.
892 |       // For example, foo references bar in: function foo() {bar=5}.
893 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarOrSimpleAssignLhs(n, parent)) {
894 |         if (referring != null) {
895 |           recordReference(referringName, name, RefType.REGULAR);
896 |         }
897 |         return;
898 |       }
899 | 
900 |       if (nodesToKeep.contains(n)) {
901 |         NameInformation functionScope = getEnclosingFunctionDependencyScope(t);
902 |         if (functionScope != null) {
903 |           recordReference(functionScope.name, name, RefType.REGULAR);
904 |         } else {
905 |           recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
906 |         }
907 |       } else if (referring != null) {
908 |         if (!maybeRecordAlias(name, parent, referring, referringName)) {
909 |           RefType depType = referring.onlyAffectsClassDef ?
910 |               RefType.INHERITANCE : RefType.REGULAR;
911 |           recordReference(referringName, name, depType);
912 |         }
913 |       } else {
914 |         // No named dependency scope found.  Unfortunately that might
915 |         // mean that the expression is a child of an function expression
916 |         // or assignment with a complex lhs.  In those cases,
917 |         // protect this node by creating a reference to WINDOW.
918 |         for (Node ancestor : n.getAncestors()) {
919 |           if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(ancestor) ||
920 |               ancestor.isFunction()) {
921 |             recordReference(WINDOW, name, RefType.REGULAR);
922 |             break;
923 |           }
924 |         }
925 |       }
926 |     }

===== M0869 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1737-1772
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1737 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1738 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1739 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1740 |     }
1741 | 
1742 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1743 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1744 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1745 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1746 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1747 |       skipEOLs();
1748 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1749 |         next();
1750 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1751 |       }
1752 |     }
1753 | 
1754 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1755 | 
1756 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1757 |       next();
1758 |       skipEOLs();
1759 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1760 |       if (memberType != null) {
1761 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1762 | 
1763 |         skipEOLs();
1764 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1765 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1766 |         }
1767 | 
1768 |         next();
1769 |       }
1770 |     }
1771 |     return typeNameNode;
1772 |   }

     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect an
     * assignment to a global name in global scope.

Method code:
544 |     void handleSetFromGlobal(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name,
545 |                              boolean isPropAssign, Name.Type type) {

===== M0889 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GatherSideEffectSubexpressionsCallback.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GatherSideEffectSubexpressionsCallback.java
Original range: 221-260
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the list of subtrees whose root nodes have side effects.
   *
   * <p>If the current subtree's root has side effects this method should
   * call accumulator.keepSubTree and return 'false' to add the
   * subtree to the result list and avoid avoid traversing the nodes children.
   *
   * <p>Branching nodes whose then or else branch contain side effects
   * must be simplified by doing a recursive traversal; this method
   * should call the appropriate accumulator 'keepSimplified' method
   * and return 'false' to stop the regular traversal.
   */
Method code:
221 |   @Override
222 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
223 |       NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
224 |     if (FORBIDDEN_TYPES.contains(node.getType()) ||
225 |         NodeUtil.isControlStructure(node)) {
226 |       throw new IllegalArgumentException(
227 |           Token.name(node.getType()) + " nodes are not supported.");
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     // Do not recurse into nested functions.
231 |     if (node.isFunction()) {
232 |       return false;
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // simplify and maybe keep hook expression.
236 |     if (node.isHook()) {
237 |       return processHook(node);
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // simplify and maybe keep AND/OR expression.
241 |     if ((node.isAnd()) || (node.isOr())) {
242 |       return processShortCircuitExpression(node);
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     if (!NodeUtil.nodeTypeMayHaveSideEffects(node)) {
246 |       return true;
247 |     } else {
248 | 
249 |       // Node type suggests that the expression has side effects.
250 | 
251 |       if (node.isCall()) {
252 |         return processFunctionCall(node);
253 |       } else if (node.isNew()) {
254 |         return processConstructorCall(node);
255 |       } else {
256 |         accumulator.keepSubTree(node);
257 |         return false;
258 |       }
259 |     }
260 |   }

232 |     @Override
233 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

235 |         String name = n.getString();
236 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
237 |           return;

241 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {

277 |               "The name %s is not consistently annotated as constant.", name);

279 |       }
280 |     }

/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     PrototypeObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
375 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
377 | 
378 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 | 
382 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }
387 | 
388 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
389 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
390 |       if (superClass != null) {
391 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (isInterface()) {
395 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

===== M0899 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1490-1537
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1490 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1491 |     if (type != node.getType()
1492 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1493 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1494 |       return false;
1495 |     }
1496 | 
1497 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1498 |       return false;
1499 |     }
1500 | 
1501 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1502 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1503 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1504 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1505 |         return false;
1506 |       }
1507 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1508 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1509 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1510 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1511 |         return false;
1512 |       }
1513 | 
1514 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1515 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1516 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1517 |         return false;
1518 |       }
1519 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1520 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1521 |         return false;
1522 |       }
1523 |     }
1524 | 
1525 |     if (recurse) {
1526 |       Node n, n2;
1527 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1528 |            n != null;
1529 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1530 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1531 |           return false;
1532 |         }
1533 |       }
1534 |     }
1535 | 
1536 |     return true;
1537 |   }

===== M0911 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 495-542
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
495 |     private void extractForInitializer(
496 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
497 | 
498 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
499 |         next = c.getNext();
500 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
501 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
502 |         switch (c.getType()) {
503 |           case Token.LABEL:
504 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
505 |             break;
506 |           case Token.FOR:
507 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
508 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
509 |               if (first.isVar()) {
510 |                 // Transform:
511 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
512 |                 // to:
513 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
514 |                 Node newStatement = first;
515 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
516 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
517 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
518 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
519 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
520 |               }
521 |             } else if (!c.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
522 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
523 |               Node empty = IR.empty();
524 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
525 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
526 | 
527 |               Node newStatement;
528 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
529 |               // but are handled differently.
530 |               if (init.isVar()) {
531 |                 newStatement = init;
532 |               } else {
533 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
534 |               }
535 | 
536 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
537 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
538 |             }
539 |             break;
540 |         }
541 |       }
542 |     }

===== M0913 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 336-390
Documentation:

Method code:
336 |   protected int getChar() {
337 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
338 |       cursor++;
339 |       --ungetCursor;
340 |       if (charno == -1) {
341 |         charno = getOffset();
342 |       }
343 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
344 |     }
345 | 
346 |     for(;;) {
347 |       int c;
348 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
349 |         hitEOF = true;
350 |         if (charno == -1) {
351 |           charno = getOffset();
352 |         }
353 |         return EOF_CHAR;
354 |       }
355 |       cursor++;
356 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
357 | 
358 | 
359 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
360 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
361 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
362 |           continue;
363 |         }
364 |         lineEndChar = -1;
365 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
366 |         lineno++;
367 |       }
368 | 
369 |       if (c <= 127) {
370 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
371 |           lineEndChar = c;
372 |           c = '\n';
373 |         }
374 |       } else {
375 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
376 |           continue;
377 |         }
378 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
379 |           lineEndChar = c;
380 |           c = '\n';
381 |         }
382 |       }
383 | 
384 |       if (charno == -1) {
385 |         charno = getOffset();
386 |       }
387 | 
388 |       return c;
389 |     }
390 |   }

595 |     @Override
596 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
597 | 
598 |       // Record global variable and function declarations
599 |       if (t.inGlobalScope()) {
600 |         if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
601 |           NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, n, parent);
602 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(ns);

609 |             recordSet(nameInfo.name, nameNode);

612 |           NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, n, parent);
613 |           if (ns != null) {
614 |             recordSet(ns.name, n);

621 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
622 |

624 |         if (ns != null) {
625 |           if (ns.isPrototype) {
626 |             recordPrototypeSet(ns.prototypeClass, ns.prototypeProperty, n);
627 |           } else {
628 |             recordSet(ns.name, nameNode);
629 |           }
630 |         }
631 |       } else if (n.isCall()) {
632 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
633 |         NameInformation ns = createNameInformation(t, nameNode, n);
634 |         if (ns != null && ns.onlyAffectsClassDef) {
635 |           JsName name = getName(ns.name, false);
636 |           if (name != null) {
637 |           refNodes.add(new ClassDefiningFunctionNode(

Method code:
229 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
230 |     this.options = options;
231 |     if (errorManager == null) {
232 |       if (outStream == null) {
233 |         setErrorManager(
234 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
235 |       } else {
236 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
237 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
238 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
239 |         setErrorManager(printer);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 | 
243 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
244 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = true;
246 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
247 |       options.checkTypes = false;
248 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
249 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
250 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
251 |       // parser type warnings.
252 |       options.setWarningLevel(
253 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
254 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
255 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
256 |     }
257 | 
258 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
259 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
260 |       options.setWarningLevel(
261 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
262 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
266 |       options.setWarningLevel(
267 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
268 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
269 |     }
270 | 
271 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
272 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
273 |     guards.add(
274 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
275 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
276 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
277 | 
278 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
279 | 
280 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
281 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
282 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
283 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
284 |     // error off.
285 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
286 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
287 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
288 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
289 |     }
290 | 
291 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
292 |   }

===== M0948 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Denormalize.maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Denormalize.java
Original range: 65-127
Documentation:
/**
   * Collapse VARs and EXPR_RESULT node into FOR loop initializers where
   * possible.
   */
Method code:
65 |   private void maybeCollapseIntoForStatements(Node n, Node parent) {
66 |     // Only SCRIPT, BLOCK, and LABELs can have FORs that can be collapsed into.
67 |     // LABELs are not supported here.
68 |     if (parent == null || !NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)) {
69 |       return;
70 |     }
71 | 
72 |     // Is the current node something that can be in a for loop initializer?
73 |     if (!n.isExprResult() && !n.isVar()) {
74 |       return;
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     // Is the next statement a valid FOR?
78 |     Node nextSibling = n.getNext();
79 |     if (nextSibling == null) {
80 |       return;
81 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isForIn(nextSibling)) {
82 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
83 |       Node forVar = forNode.getFirstChild();
84 |       if (forVar.isName()
85 |           && n.isVar() && n.hasOneChild()) {
86 |         Node name = n.getFirstChild();
87 |         if (!name.hasChildren()
88 |             && forVar.getString().equals(name.getString())) {
89 |           // Ok, the names match, and the var declaration does not have an
90 |           // initializer. Move it into the loop.
91 |           parent.removeChild(n);
92 |           forNode.replaceChild(forVar, n);
93 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
94 |         }
95 |       }
96 |     } else if (nextSibling.isFor()
97 |         && nextSibling.getFirstChild().isEmpty()) {
98 | 
99 |       // Does the current node contain an in operator?  If so, embedding
100 |       // the expression in a for loop can cause some Javascript parsers (such
101 |       // as the Playstation 3's browser based on Access's NetFront
102 |       // browser) to fail to parse the code.
103 |       // See bug 1778863 for details.
104 |       if (NodeUtil.containsType(n, Token.IN)) {
105 |         return;
106 |       }
107 | 
108 |       // Move the current node into the FOR loop initializer.
109 |       Node forNode = nextSibling;
110 |       Node oldInitializer = forNode.getFirstChild();
111 |       parent.removeChild(n);
112 | 
113 |       Node newInitializer;
114 |       if (n.isVar()) {
115 |         newInitializer = n;
116 |       } else {
117 |         // Extract the expression from EXPR_RESULT node.
118 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
119 |         newInitializer = n.getFirstChild();
120 |         n.removeChild(newInitializer);
121 |       }
122 | 
123 |       forNode.replaceChild(oldInitializer, newInitializer);
124 | 
125 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
126 |     }
127 |   }

===== M0970 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.findDeclaredNames(Node,Node,Renamer)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 212-229
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
212 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
213 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
214 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
215 |     if (parent == null
216 |         || !parent.isFunction()
217 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
218 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
220 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
221 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
222 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
223 |       }
224 | 
225 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
226 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
227 |       }
228 |     }
229 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java

/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
781 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
782 |     if (item == null) {
783 |       return null;
784 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
785 |       return item.getNext();
786 |     } else {
787 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
788 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
789 |         return item.chain(result);
790 |       } else {
791 |         return item;
792 |       }
793 |     }
794 |   }

===== M0982 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SourceFile.getLine(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SourceFile.java
Original range: 205-250
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the source line for the indicated line number.
   *
   * @param lineNumber the line number, 1 being the first line of the file.
   * @return The line indicated. Does not include the newline at the end
   *     of the file. Returns {@code null} if it does not exist,
   *     or if there was an IO exception.
   */
Method code:
205 |   public String getLine(int lineNumber) {
206 |     String js = "";
207 |     try {
208 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, this is optimized for few warnings.
209 |       // This is probably the right trade-off, but will be slow if there
210 |       // are lots of warnings in one file.
211 |       js = getCode();
212 |     } catch (IOException e) {
213 |       return null;
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     int pos = 0;
217 |     int startLine = 1;
218 | 
219 |     // If we've saved a previous offset and it's for a line less than the
220 |     // one we're searching for, then start at that point.
221 |     if (lineNumber >= lastLine) {
222 |       pos = lastOffset;
223 |       startLine = lastLine;
224 |     }
225 | 
226 |     for (int n = startLine; n < lineNumber; n++) {
227 |       int nextpos = js.indexOf('\n', pos);
228 |       if (nextpos == -1) {
229 |         return null;
230 |       }
231 |       pos = nextpos + 1;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     // Remember this offset for the next search we do.
235 |     lastOffset = pos;
236 |     lastLine = lineNumber;
237 | 
238 |     if (js.indexOf('\n', pos) == -1) {
239 |       // If next new line cannot be found, there are two cases
240 |       // 1. pos already reaches the end of file, then null should be returned
241 |       // 2. otherwise, return the contents between pos and the end of file.
242 |       if (pos >= js.length()) {
243 |         return null;
244 |       } else {
245 |         return js.substring(pos, js.length());
246 |       }
247 |     } else {
248 |       return js.substring(pos, js.indexOf('\n', pos));
249 |     }
250 |   }

===== M1002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1610-1626
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1610 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1611 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1612 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1613 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1614 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1615 |         next();
1616 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1617 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1618 |           next();
1619 |         }
1620 |         skipEOLs();
1621 |         token = next();
1622 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1623 |       }
1624 |     }
1625 |     return typeExpr;
1626 |   }

===== M1010 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 156-260
Documentation:

Method code:
156 |     @Override
157 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
158 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
159 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
160 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
161 |       if (inExterns && node.isName() && parent.isParamList()) {
162 |         return;
163 |       }
164 | 
165 |       Definition def =
166 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
167 |       if (def != null) {
168 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
169 |         if (name != null) {
170 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
171 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
172 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
173 |               !rValue.isFunction()) {
174 | 
175 |             // Unhandled complex expression
176 |             Definition unknownDef =
177 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
178 |             def = unknownDef;
179 |           }
180 | 
181 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
182 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
183 |           if (inExterns) {
184 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
185 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
186 | 
187 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
188 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
189 | 
190 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
191 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
192 |             // literal key.
193 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
194 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
195 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
196 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
197 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
198 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
199 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
200 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
201 |                   }
202 |                 }
203 |               }
204 | 
205 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
206 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
207 |               }
208 |             }
209 |           }
210 | 
211 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
212 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
213 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
214 |                                                    def,
215 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
216 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
217 |                                                    inExterns));
218 |         }
219 |       }
220 | 
221 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && parent.isExprResult()) {
222 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
223 |         if (name != null) {
224 | 
225 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
226 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
227 | 
228 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
229 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
230 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
231 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
232 | 
233 |           boolean dropStub = false;
234 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
235 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
236 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
237 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
238 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
239 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
240 |                   dropStub = true;
241 |                   break;
242 |                 }
243 |               }
244 |             }
245 |           }
246 | 
247 |           if (!dropStub) {
248 |             // Incomplete definition
249 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
250 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
251 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
252 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
253 |                                                      definition,
254 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
255 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
256 |                                                      inExterns));
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }

===== M1011 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.AstValidator.validateChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/AstValidator.java
Original range: 774-788
Documentation:

Method code:
774 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
775 |     boolean valid = false;
776 |     if (i == 0) {
777 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
778 |     } else if (i == 1) {
779 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
780 |     } else {
781 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
782 |     }
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }
788 |   }

===== M1014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 609-670
Documentation:

Method code:
609 |     @Override
610 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
611 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
612 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
613 |       if (name == null) {
614 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
615 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
616 |           errorReporter.error(
617 |             "unnamed function statement",
618 |             sourceName,
619 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
620 | 
621 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
622 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
623 |         }
624 |         name = new Name();
625 |         name.setIdentifier("");
626 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
627 |       }
628 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
629 |       Node newName = transform(name);
630 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
631 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
632 |         // declaration.
633 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
634 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
635 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
636 |         // same line as the function keyword.
637 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
638 |             functionNode.getLp();
639 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
640 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
641 |       }
642 | 
643 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
644 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
645 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
646 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
647 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
648 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
649 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
650 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
651 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
652 |       if (fnName != null) {
653 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
654 |       } else {
655 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
656 |       }
657 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
658 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
659 | 
660 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
661 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
662 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
663 |       }
664 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
665 | 
666 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
667 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
668 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
669 |      return node;
670 |     }

===== M1028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M1029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 135-147
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
135 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
136 |     if (node.isName()) {
137 |       String name = node.getString();
138 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
139 |         return name;
140 |       } else {
141 |         return null;
142 |       }
143 |     } else if (node.isGetProp()) {
144 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
145 |     }
146 |     return null;
147 |   }

===== M1030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M1064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M1072 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 164-180
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
165 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.isCall());
166 | 
167 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
168 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
169 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
170 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
171 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
172 |       }
173 | 
174 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
175 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
176 |       if (first.isName() &&
177 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
178 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
179 |       }
180 |     }

===== M1074 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 392-436
Documentation:

Method code:
392 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
393 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
394 |       cursor++;
395 |       --ungetCursor;
396 |       if (charno == -1) {
397 |         charno = getOffset();
398 |       }
399 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     for(;;) {
403 |       int c;
404 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
405 |         hitEOF = true;
406 |         if (charno == -1) {
407 |           charno = getOffset();
408 |         }
409 |         return EOF_CHAR;
410 |       }
411 |       cursor++;
412 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
413 | 
414 | 
415 |       if (c <= 127) {
416 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
417 |           lineEndChar = c;
418 |           c = '\n';
419 |         }
420 |       } else {
421 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
422 |           continue;
423 |         }
424 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
425 |           lineEndChar = c;
426 |           c = '\n';
427 |         }
428 |       }
429 | 
430 |       if (charno == -1) {
431 |         charno = getOffset();
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       return c;
435 |     }
436 |   }

315 |   private String formatNodePosition(Node n) {
316 |     if (n == null) {
317 |       return MISSING_SOURCE + "\n";
318 |     }
319 | 
320 |     int lineNumber = n.getLineno();
321 |     int columnNumber = n.getCharno();
322 |     String src = compiler.getSourceLine(sourceName, lineNumber);
323 |     if (src == null) {
324 |       src = MISSING_SOURCE;

327 |         + src + "\n";

Documentation:
/**
     * Do normalizations that introduce new siblings or parents.
     */
Method code:
433 |     private void doStatementNormalizations(
434 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
435 |       if (n.isLabel()) {
436 |         normalizeLabels(n);
437 |       }
438 | 
439 |       // Only inspect the children of SCRIPTs, BLOCKs and LABELs, as all these
440 |       // are the only legal place for VARs and FOR statements.

446 |       // are the only legal place for VARs.

448 |         splitVarDeclarations(n);
449 |       }
